We evaluate these conditions across four task types--summarization, planning, explanation, and coding--using three LLM systems: ChatGPT, Claude, and Grok. Each output is scored with a unified rubric covering task completion, correctness, compliance, and clarity. Checklist-improved prompts achieved the highest mean rubric score, 7.50 out of 8, compared with 5.67 for raw prompts and 6.67 for clarifying-question prompts. Checklist prompts also produced the best quality-effort tradeoff, using fewer average tokens than both raw and clarifying prompts. These results suggest that a simple prompt checklist can improve LLM responses while reducing unnecessary interaction.

</details>


### [39] [ClinSeekAgent: Automating Multimodal Evidence Seeking for Agentic Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20176)
*Juncheng Wu,Letian Zhang,Yuhan Wang,Haoqin Tu,Hardy Chen,Zijun Wang,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClinSeekAgent是一个面向临床决策的自动化多模态证据检索智能体框架，将范式从被动证据消费转变为主动证据获取，在文本和多模态任务上均显著提升模型性能，并通过蒸馏技术使小型模型接近前沿大模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM和智能体系统在临床决策支持中大多假设证据已被预先整理并提供给模型，而真实临床工作流需要智能体主动从异构数据源中搜索、迭代规划并综合多模态证据，因此需要一种从被动证据消费转向主动证据获取的新范式。

Method: 提出ClinSeekAgent框架，包含三个核心能力：(1) 通过查询医学知识库、处理原始电子健康记录(EHR)和调用医学影像工具主动收集证据；(2) 随着新信息出现迭代优化假设；(3) 将收集的证据整合为基于事实的临床决策。同时构建ClinSeek-Bench基准测试，区分预选证据推理和自动化证据检索两种任务场景。

Result: 在纯文本EHR任务上，Claude Opus 4.6的F1从60.0提升至63.2，MiniMax M2.5从43.1提升至47.3；在多模态任务上，Claude Opus 4.6的F1从47.5提升至62.6（+15.1）。蒸馏得到的ClinSeek-35B-A3B在AgentEHR-Bench上达到34.0平均F1，比基线提升+11.9分，接近Claude Opus 4.6水平。

Conclusion: ClinSeekAgent验证了主动证据检索在临床决策中的有效性，同时作为训练管道可通过蒸馏将智能体轨迹迁移至小型开源模型，为实际临床部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and agentic systems have shown promise for clinical decision support, but existing works largely assume that evidence has already been curated and handed to the model. Real-world clinical workflows instead require agents to actively seek, iteratively plan, and synthesize multimodal evidence from heterogeneous sources. In this paper, we introduce ClinSeekAgent, an automated agentic framework for dynamic multimodal evidence seeking that shifts the paradigm from passive evidence consumption to active evidence acquisition. Given only a clinical query and access to raw data sources, ClinSeekAgent gathers evidence by querying medical knowledge bases, navigating raw EHRs, and invoking medical imaging tools; refines its hypotheses as new information emerges; and integrates the collected evidence into grounded clinical decisions. ClinSeekAgent serves both as an inference-time agent for frontier LLMs and as a training-time pipeline for distilling high-quality agent trajectories into compact open-source models. To validate its inference-time effectiveness, we construct ClinSeek-Bench, which pairs Curated Input reasoning from fixed pre-selected evidence with Automated Evidence-Seeking over raw clinical data. On text-only EHR tasks, ClinSeekAgent improves Claude Opus 4.6 from 60.0 to 63.2 overall F1 and MiniMax M2.5 from 43.1 to 47.3, with positive risk-prediction gains in 7 out of 9 evaluated host models. On multimodal tasks, ClinSeekAgent improves Claude Opus 4.6 from 47.5 to 62.6 (+15.1); all evaluated models improve across the three CXR-related task groups. We further validate ClinSeekAgent as a training pipeline by distilling agentic evidence-seeking trajectories into ClinSeek-35B-A3B, which achieves 34.0 average F1 on existing AgentEHR-Bench, improving over its Qwen3.5-35B-A3B baseline by +11.9 points and approaching Claude Opus 4.6.

</details>


### [40] [From Seeing to Thinking: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning Improves Post-Training of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20177)
*Juncheng Wu,Hardy Chen,Haoqin Tu,Xianfeng Tang,Freda Shi,Hui Liu,Hanqing Lu,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了视觉-语言模型中感知与推理的相互作用，提出分阶段训练策略（视觉感知→视觉推理→文本推理），发现视觉感知是推理的基础瓶颈，通过强化学习和分阶段训练可同时提升感知能力和推理效率（推理准确率+1.5%，推理长度缩短20.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM虽然在长链推理上取得进展，但性能瓶颈主要来自视觉感知不足而非推理能力本身，因此需要系统研究感知与推理的关系并设计针对性训练策略。

Method: 将VLM后训练分解为三个独立阶段：视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理，分别使用专门训练数据；采用分阶段训练代替混合训练，并用强化学习替代基于描述的监督微调来优化视觉感知。

Result: 分阶段训练相比混合训练在多个VLM上一致提升感知与推理性能，推理准确率提升1.5%的同时推理轨迹缩短20.8%，在WeMath和RealWorldQA上分别提升5.2%和3.7%。

Conclusion: 视觉感知需要针对性训练且是推理的基础，分阶段训练是一种独立于传统难度递进课程的新维度，两者结合可产生叠加收益，该方法在开源VLM中达到领先水平。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) emphasize long chain-of-thought reasoning; yet, we find that their performance on visual tasks is primarily limited by a lack of visual perception as opposed to reasoning itself. In this work, we systematically study the interplay between perception and reasoning in VLM post-training by decomposing their capabilities into three separate training stages: visual perception, visual reasoning, and textual reasoning, incorporating specialized training data. We demonstrate that visual perception (a) requires targeted optimization with specialized data; (b) serves as a fundamental scaffold that should be solidified through staged training before refining visual reasoning; and (c) is more effectively learned via RL than caption-based SFT. Our experiments across multiple VLMs demonstrate that staged training consistently improves both visual perception and reasoning performance over merged training. Notably, models trained with our approach achieve 1.5% higher reasoning accuracy with 20.8% shorter reasoning traces, suggesting that superior perception reduces the need for excessive reasoning. Furthermore, we show that this capability-based staging represents a new curriculum dimension orthogonal to traditional difficulty-based curricula, and combining both yields further additive gains. Our staged-training models achieve superior performance among open-weight VLMs, establishing advanced results on several visual math and perception (e.g., +5.2% on WeMath and +3.7% on RealWorldQA) tasks compared with the base counterpart.

</details>


### [41] [TIDE: Efficient and Lossless MoE Diffusion LLM Inference with I/O-aware Expert Offload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20179)
*Zhiben Chen,Youpeng Zhao,Yang Sui,Jun Wang,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDE，一种面向扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的新型资源高效推理系统，利用块内扩散过程中专家激活的时间稳定性，通过基于间隔的专家刷新策略和数学规划调度，在不需模型训练的情况下实现无损加速，在GPU-CPU系统上相比基线获得最高1.5倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）虽在硬件利用和双向上下文方面优于自回归模型，但随着混合专家（MoE）架构的扩展，在资源受限设备上部署仍面临挑战，现有自回归方法存在I/O开销过大或计算瓶颈问题。

Method: 利用块内扩散过程中专家激活的时间稳定性，提出基于间隔的专家刷新策略（I/O感知方式更新专家放置），并将推理调度建模为数学规划问题，求解最优间隔以最小化I/O流量和CPU计算。

Result: 在单个GPU-CPU系统上，TIDE在LLaDA2.0-mini和LLaDA2.0-flash模型上分别比基线方法提升1.4倍和1.5倍吞吐量。

Conclusion: TIDE是一种无需模型训练的无损优化方法，为dLLM推理提供"免费午餐"式的加速效果。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, offering better hardware utilization and bidirectional context through parallel block-level decoding. However, as dLLMs continue to scale up with mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, their deployment on resource-constrained devices remains an open challenge. Existing AR-based methods often incur either prohibitive I/O overhead or significant compute bottlenecks. In this work, we propose TIDE, a novel resource-efficient inference system that leverages the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block. Specifically, we leverage the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block and introduce an interval-based expert refresh strategy that updates the expert placement in an I/O-aware fashion. To ensure optimal performance, we formulate the inference scheduling as a mathematical programming problem, solving for the optimal interval that minimizes I/O traffic and CPU computation. Most importantly, TIDE is a lossless optimization that requires no model training, providing a "free lunch" acceleration for dLLM inference. In a single GPU-CPU system, we demonstrate that TIDE achieves up to 1.4$\times$ and 1.5$\times$ throughput improvements over prior baselines on LLaDA2.0-mini and LLaDA2.0-flash models, respectively.

</details>


### [42] [IMLJD: A Computational Dataset for Indian Matrimonial Litigation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19346)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IMLJD数据集，包含3,613份印度婚姻纠纷法院判决，发现最高法院的驳回成功率（57.6%）显著高于卡纳塔克邦高等法院（39.7%），经时间匹配后差距扩大至19.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究印度婚姻纠纷案件（涉及IPC第498A条、反家庭暴力法及CrPC第482条）的司法判决结果，构建一个结构化、开放的数据集，以揭示不同层级法院之间的裁决差异。

Method: 收集2000-2024年印度最高法院（1,474件）和2018-2024年卡纳塔克邦高等法院（2,139件）共3,613份判决书，标注结构化结果标签、元数据指标，并构建知识图谱。采用时间匹配方法（2018-2024年）进行稳健性检验。

Result: 最高法院驳回成功率57.6%，卡纳塔克邦高等法院39.7%。时间匹配后最高法院59.3%，差距扩大至19.6个百分点，结论具有稳健性。

Conclusion: 印度最高法院在婚姻纠纷案件中显著更倾向于支持驳回申请，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: We present IMLJD, an open dataset of 3,613 Indian court judgments covering matrimonial disputes under IPC Section 498A, the Protection of Women from Domestic Violence Act, and CrPC Section 482. The dataset covers the Supreme Court of India from 2000 to 2024 (1,474 cases) and the Karnataka High Court from 2018 to 2024 (2,139 cases), with structured outcome labels, metadata-derived indicators, and a knowledge graph. We find that 57.6% of quashing petitions succeed at the Supreme Court level compared to 39.7% at the Karnataka High Court level. On a matched 2018 to 2024 period, the SC quash rate is 59.3%, widening the differential to 19.6 percentage points and confirming the finding is robust to temporal adjustment. The dataset, code, and knowledge graph are released openly at https://github.com/joyboseroy/imljd and https://huggingface.co/datasets/joyboseroy/imljd.

</details>


### [43] [EmbGen: Teaching with Reassembled Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19394)
*Arun K Lenin,Kai Rouse,Andrea Nicastro,Anna Leontjeva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EmbGen是一种利用嵌入相似性从语料库中生成多样化QA对的合成数据管线，在高度异构数据集上比强基线提升12.5%~88.9%的Binary Accuracy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对小规模指令微调模型的领域适应依赖昂贵的监督微调，合成数据生成方法存在输出同质化、无法捕捉跨文档依赖的问题。

Method: 将语料库分解为实体-描述对，通过嵌入相似性推断语义结构重新组装，并利用邻近采样、簇内采样和簇间采样结合簇专用系统提示生成QA对。

Result: 在5M和20M token预算下，EmbGen在最高异构数据集上Binary Accuracy分别比最强基线提升12.5%和88.9%，在低异构数据集上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: EmbGen通过结构化语义分解与多样化采样策略，有效提升了合成数据在异构领域上的质量。

Abstract: Adapting small instruction-tuned models to specialized domains often relies on supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on curated instruction-response examples, which is expensive to collect at scale. Synthetic training examples generated by a teacher LLM from a domain corpus can reduce this cost, but existing pipelines can produce homogenized outputs and do not consistently capture cross-passage or cross-document dependencies. We introduce EmbGen, a synthetic data generation pipeline that decomposes a corpus into entity-description pairs, reassembles them using semantic structure inferred from embedding similarity, and then generates question-answer (QA) pairs via proximity, intra-cluster, and inter-cluster sampling with cluster-specialized system prompts. We evaluate EmbGen against EntiGraph, InstructLab and Knowledge-Instruct on three datasets of varied semantic heterogeneity, under fixed token budgets (5 and 20 million tokens). We use lexical overlap metrics, an LLM-as-a-judge rubric, and Binary Accuracy, a composed metric combining Factual Accuracy and Completeness for evaluation. EmbGen improves Binary Accuracy on the most heterogeneous dataset by 12.5% at 5M and 88.9% at 20M tokens budget, relative to the strongest baseline, while remaining competitive across other datasets with lower heterogeneity.

</details>


### [44] [Base Models Look Human To AI Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19516)
*Yixuan Even Xu,Ziqian Zhong,Aditi Raghunathan,Fei Fang,J. Zico Kolter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，商用AI文本检测器（如GPTZero、Pangram）对基础模型生成的文本倾向于判定为"人类撰写"，而对指令微调模型生成的文本则能准确识别。基于此，提出了一种名为HIP（迭代释义人类化）的检测器无关方法，通过微调基础模型为释义器并迭代应用，在语义保留与检测规避间取得了更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前商用AI文本检测器在教育和学术诚信领域广泛使用，但其检测机制存在盲点：对基础模型生成文本的识别能力不足。论文旨在揭示这一缺陷并开发更有效的规避方法，同时推动检测器设计的改进。

Method: 提出HIP（Humanization by Iterative Paraphrasing）流水线：对基础模型进行最小程度的微调，将其转化为释义器，然后迭代应用该释义器对文本进行改写，以绕过检测器。

Result: 在Llama-3和Qwen-3系列模型（0.6B至70B参数规模）上，HIP在语义保留和商用检测器规避之间取得了更优的权衡，且能一致性地提升检测器对文本的"人类似然度"评分。

Conclusion: 当前检测器更多是在追踪指令微调和局部上下文的"伪影"，而非捕捉机器生成文本的不变特征。这呼吁未来的检测器设计需要更明确地对这些因素进行建模。

Abstract: As AI-generated text enters the real-world at scale, institutions increasingly use commercial AI-text detectors, especially in education and academic-integrity workflows. We report a surprising empirical finding about such systems: when evaluated by GPTZero and Pangram, generated text from base models is often judged overwhelmingly human, whereas text generated by their instruction-tuned counterparts is not. Building on this observation, we propose Humanization by Iterative Paraphrasing (HIP), a detector-agnostic pipeline that minimally fine-tunes a base model into a paraphraser and applies it iteratively. Compared with the baselines we test, HIP yields a stronger trade-off between semantic preservation and detector evasion on commercial detectors. Across Llama-3 and Qwen-3 families, spanning model sizes from 0.6B to 70B, HIP consistently improves detector human-likeness. Our findings suggest that current detectors are tracking artifacts of instruction tuning and local context more than any invariant notion of machine-generated text. This, in turn, calls for detector designs that model these factors more explicitly.

</details>


### [45] [CopT: Contrastive On-Policy Thinking with Continuous Spaces for General and Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20075)
*Dachuan Shi,Hanlin Zhu,Xiangchi Yuan,Wanjia Zhao,Kejing Xia,Wen Xiao,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CopT提出了一种反转思考与回答顺序的推理流程，先产生草稿答案再基于策略思考进行修正，并通过对比连续嵌入与离散输入的KL散度估计答案可靠性，在数学、编码和智能体推理任务上实现最高23%的准确率提升和57%的token节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CoT将思考视为回答的前提，但模型在能直接给出答案时仍进行冗长推理（表演性推理），导致延迟和额外token开销，因此需要更高效的推理范式。

Method: CopT反转推理顺序：先产生草稿答案，再基于策略思考进行反思修正。利用推理时对比验证器，通过对比同一token在离散输入与连续嵌入下的支持度，计算序列级反向KL散度作为答案可靠性估计，并在不可靠时动态控制草稿答案可见性进行进一步思考。

Result: 在数学、编码和智能体推理任务上，CopT相比标准CoT最高提升23%的峰值准确率，同时减少最高57%的token使用量，且在同等或更高准确率下均优于基线。

Conclusion: CopT通过反转推理顺序和对比验证机制，在不需额外训练的情况下显著提升LLM推理效率与准确性，反向KL估计量与答案相关不确定性之间的理论联系解释了其有效性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is a standard approach for eliciting reasoning capabilities from large language models (LLMs). However, the common CoT paradigm treats thinking as a prerequisite for answering, which can delay access to plausible answers and incur unnecessary token costs even when the model is able to identify an answer before extended thinking, a behavior known as performative reasoning. In this paper, we introduce CopT, a reformulated reasoning pipeline that reverses the usual order of thinking and answering. Instead of thinking before answering, CopT first elicits a draft answer and then invokes subsequent on-policy thinking conditioned on its own draft answer for reflection and correction. To assess whether the draft answer should be trusted, CopT recasts continuous embeddings as inference-time contrastive verifiers. Specifically, it contrasts the model's support for the same generated tokens under discrete-token inputs and continuous-embedding inputs, yielding a sequence-level reverse KL estimator for answer reliability. Our analysis shows that under certain assumptions, the expected estimate equals the mutual information between the unresolved latent state and the emitted answer token, explaining why it captures answer-relevant uncertainty rather than arbitrary uncertainty in the latent state. When the answer is deemed insufficiently reliable, CopT performs further on-policy thinking, where a second KL estimator dynamically controls draft-answer visibility, preserving useful partial information while reducing the risk of being misled by unreliable content. Across mathematics, coding, and agentic reasoning tasks, CopT improves peak accuracy by up to 23% and reduces token usage by up to 57% at comparable or higher accuracy, without any additional training. The code is available at https://github.com/sdc17/CopT.

</details>


### [46] [ThoughtTrace: Understanding User Thoughts in Real-World LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20087)
*Chuanyang Jin,Binze Li,Haopeng Xie,Cathy Mengying Fang,Tianjian Li,Shayne Longpre,Hongxiang Gu,Maximillian Chen,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ThoughtTrace是首个大规模配对真实人机对话与用户内心想法（发送提示的原因及对助手回复的反应）的数据集，包含1,058名用户、2,155次对话、17,058轮次及10,174条想法标注，证明想法是一种独立于对话内容的新数据模态，可用于改进用户行为预测和个性化助手训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话数据集仅记录用户说什么，而不记录用户想什么，缺乏对用户真实意图、目标、偏好和需求的认知理解，阻碍了AI助手深度理解用户的能力。

Method: 构建ThoughtTrace数据集：从20种语言模型中收集1,058名用户的2,155次真实对话，标注用户自述的思考内容（发送提示的原因和对助手的反应），并通过语义分析、模型推理对比等方法验证想法与消息的差异性。

Result: ThoughtTrace捕获了长跨度、主题多样的交互；想法与消息在语义上具有区分性；前沿LLM难以从上下文中推断出用户想法；想法内容多样且与对话阶段相关；想法作为推理上下文可改进用户行为预测；想法引导的改写可为个性化助手训练提供细粒度对齐信号。

Conclusion: ThoughtTrace将用户想法确立为研究人机交互认知动态的新数据模态，为构建能更好理解和适应用户潜在目标、偏好与需求的助手提供了基础。

Abstract: Conversational AI has now reached billions of users, yet existing datasets capture only what people say, not what they think. We introduce ThoughtTrace, the first large-scale dataset that pairs real-world multi-turn human--AI conversations with users' self-reported thoughts: their reasons for sending prompts and reactions to assistant responses. ThoughtTrace comprises 1,058 users, 2,155 conversations, 17,058 turns, and 10,174 thought annotations collected across 20 language models. Our analysis shows that ThoughtTrace captures long-horizon, topically diverse interactions, and that thoughts are semantically distinct from messages, difficult for frontier LLMs to infer from context, diverse in content, and tied to conversation stages. We further demonstrate the utility of thoughts for downstream modeling. First, thoughts improve user-behavior prediction as inference-time context. Second, thought-guided rewrites provide fine-grained alignment signals for training personalized assistants. Together, ThoughtTrace establishes user thoughts as a new data modality for studying the cognitive dynamics behind human--AI interaction and provides a foundation for building assistants that better understand and adapt to users' latent goals, preferences, and needs.

</details>

### [47] [Dimensional Balance Improves Large Scale Spatiotemporal Prediction Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18793)
*Jing Chen,Shixiang Pan,Yujie Fan,Haocheng Ye,Haitao Xu,Wenqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对时空模式分析中现有方法性能瓶颈和跨领域迁移性差的问题，本文通过空间和时间熵度量诊断复杂性不匹配，并提出ST-Balance自适应框架，通过低秩矩阵嵌入压缩空间维度、扩展时间窗口捕获长程依赖，在交通、气象、流行病数据集上取得显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空模式分析方法存在性能瓶颈，通常只能带来增量提升且跨领域迁移性有限，亟需一种更高效、可扩展且具有良好泛化能力的解决方案。

Method: 1. 利用空间和时间熵度量作为诊断指标，识别时空复杂性不匹配；2. 提出自适应框架ST-Balance：通过低秩矩阵嵌入压缩空间维度以保留核心结构，同时扩展时间窗口捕获长程依赖，减轻时间异质性带来的累积误差。

Result: 在城市交通、气象和流行病数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在精度上取得显著提升，且评估领域内具有广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 提出的ST-Balance框架在多种时空任务中表现优异，具有良好的跨领域迁移潜力，有望推广至更广泛的时空分析应用场景。

Abstract: Accurate spatiotemporal pattern analysis is critical in fields such as urban traffic, meteorology, and public health monitoring. However, existing methods face performance bottlenecks, typically yielding only incremental gains and often exhibiting limited cross-domain transferability. We analyze this bottleneck through spatial and temporal entropy measures, which are used as diagnostic indicators of spatiotemporal complexity mismatch rather than as guarantees that entropy alignment alone yields better forecasting. Empirically, larger mismatch is often accompanied by higher prediction uncertainty, especially under a fixed model-capacity budget. Guided by this diagnostic, we propose a scalable, adaptive framework that harmonizes spatial and temporal feature representations. Spatial dimensionality is compressed via low-rank matrix embedding to preserve essential structure, while an extended temporal horizon captures long-range dependencies and mitigates cumulative errors arising from temporal heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on urban traffic, meteorological, and epidemic datasets demonstrate substantial accuracy gains and broad applicability across the evaluated domains, suggesting that the framework is promising for a wide range of spatiotemporal tasks beyond the current study. The code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/ST-Balance/ST-Balance.

</details>


### [48] [HELLoRA: Hot Experts Layer-Level Low-Rank Adaptation for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18795)
*Jia Wei,Zhonghao Zhang,Ping Chen,Qianyang li,Yancheng Pan,Shaoxun Wang,Ziyi Qiu,Longxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HELLoRA是一种面向混合专家模型（MoE）的参数高效微调方法，通过仅对每层中最频繁激活的专家附加LoRA模块，显著减少可训练参数和计算开销，同时提升下游任务性能。与LoRA结合形成HELLoRI后，在极端参数预算下仍表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA变体主要针对密集架构，而MoE模型的稀疏激活模式尚未被充分用于高效微调。作者希望利用MoE的稀疏特性，提出一种激活感知的适配器放置策略，以更少的参数和计算资源实现更好的微调效果。

Method: HELLoRA仅对每层中激活频率最高的专家附加LoRA模块；为应对极端参数预算，进一步与LoRI结合形成HELLoRI，冻结上投影并稀疏化下投影。

Result: 在OlMoE-1B-7B、Mixtral-8x7B、DeepSeekMoE三个MoE骨干模型上，覆盖数学推理、代码生成和安全对齐三类任务，HELLoRA一致优于强PEFT基线。以OlMoE为例，相比LoRA仅用15.7%可训练参数、减少38.7%适配器FLOPs、训练吞吐量提升1.9倍、准确率提升9.2%。在DeepSeekMoE上仅用23.2%参数即超越LoRA。

Conclusion: 激活感知的适配器放置是一种有效且实用的路径，可用于扩展MoE语言模型的参数高效微调。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) dominates parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, yet most variants target dense architectures. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale parameters at near-constant per-token compute, and their sparse activation patterns create untapped opportunities for more efficient adaptation. We propose Hot-Experts Layer-level Low-Rank Adaptation (HELLoRA), which attaches LoRA modules only to the most frequently activated experts at each layer. This simple mechanism reduces trainable parameters and adapter-induced FLOPs while improving downstream performance, an effect we attribute to a form of structured regularization that preserves pretrained expert specialization. To stress-test HELLoRA under extreme parameter budgets, we further compose it with LoRI to form HELLoRI, which freezes the up-projection and sparsifies the down-projection. Across three MoE backbones, namely OlMoE-1B-7B, Mixtral-8x7B, and DeepSeekMoE, and three task families covering mathematical reasoning, code generation, and safety alignment, HELLoRA consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines. Relative to vanilla LoRA on OlMoE, HELLoRA uses 15.7% of the trainable parameters, reduces adapter FLOPs by 38.7%, achieves 1.9x the training throughput, and improves accuracy by 9.2%. On DeepSeekMoE, HELLoRA outperforms LoRA while using only 23.2% of its trainable parameters. These results demonstrate that activation-aware adapter placement is an effective and practical route to scaling PEFT for MoE language models.

</details>


### [49] [UCCI: Calibrated Uncertainty for Cost-Optimal LLM Cascade Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18796)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UCCI是一种校准优先的LLM路由方法，通过等渗回归将token级边际不确定性映射为误差概率，并利用约束成本最小化选择升级阈值，在命名实体识别任务上以31%的成本降低实现与大型模型相当的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM级联和模型路由方法大多使用未校准的置信度分数，且需要针对每个工作负载手动调整阈值，导致部署成本高且泛化性差。

Method: 提出UCCI路由框架：(1) 通过等渗回归将token级边际不确定性校准为每查询误差概率；(2) 通过约束成本最小化自动选择升级阈值；(3) 理论证明了在三个假设下基于校准分数的阈值策略是成本最优的，且等渗校准的期望校准误差(ECE)样本复杂度为O(n^{-1/3})。

Result: 在75000条命名实体识别生产工作负载上，4B和12B模型级联的UCCI在微F1=0.91时降低推理成本31%（95% CI: [27%, 35%]），ECE从0.12降至0.03，优于熵阈值、分裂共形路由和FrugalGPT风格学习阈值方法。

Conclusion: UCCI通过校准优先和自动阈值选择实现了低成本高效的路由，且所有结果基于真实模型输出和H100延迟测量，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: LLM cascades and model routing promise lower inference cost by sending easy queries to a small model and escalating hard ones to a large model, but most deployed routers use uncalibrated confidence scores and require per-workload threshold tuning. We present UCCI, a calibration-first router that maps token-level margin uncertainty to a per-query error probability via isotonic regression and selects the escalation threshold by constrained cost minimization. Under three explicit assumptions, threshold policies on the calibrated score are cost-optimal, and isotonic calibration achieves O(n^{-1/3}) sample complexity for expected calibration error (ECE). On a production named entity recognition workload of 75,000 queries served by 4B and 12B instruction-tuned LLMs on H100 GPUs, UCCI cuts inference cost by 31% (95% CI: [27%, 35%]) at micro-F1 = 0.91 while reducing ECE from 0.12 to 0.03. At the same operating point, UCCI beats entropy thresholding, split-conformal routing, and a FrugalGPT-style learned threshold. All cascade results use end-to-end routing on actual model outputs and measured H100 latency, not simulated routing from global accuracies or nominal API prices.

</details>


### [50] [Simply Stabilizing the Loop via Fully Looped Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18797)
*Rao Fu,Zixuan Yang,Jiankun Zhang,Jing Ma,Hechang Chen,Yu Li,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fully Looped Transformer，通过两种无参数修改（全循环架构和注意力注入）解决Looped Transformer在增加循环次数时的训练不稳定性问题，支持多达12次循环的稳定训练，下游任务性能提升最高13.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Looped Transformer通过重复使用相同Transformer模块，在不增加参数量和上下文长度的情况下提升性能，但增加循环次数时会出现训练不稳定。本文旨在分析并解决这一不稳定性问题。

Method: 提出Fully Looped Transformer，包含两种无参数修改：(1) 全循环架构——将循环间信号分布到所有层以缓解残差爆炸；(2) 注意力注入——重用现有注意力模块抑制梯度震荡。

Result: Fully Looped Transformer可稳定训练至12次循环，而基线模型在该设置下崩溃；在较温和场景下，下游任务平均性能提升达13.2%。

Conclusion: Fully Looped Transformer有效提升了训练稳定性、下游任务性能，并通过推理时调整循环次数提供了测试时计算预算的初步自适应能力。

Abstract: Scaling model performance typically requires increasing model size. Looped Transformer offers a compelling alternative by iteratively reusing the same Transformer blocks, trading additional computation for improved performance without increasing parameter count or context length. Because the number of loop iterations can be adjusted at inference, it also provides a natural mechanism for balancing performance and test-time compute. However, Looped Transformer still suffers from training instability when the number of loop iterations increases. Our analysis reveals that this instability stems from two sources: gradient oscillation and residual explosion. To address these two problems, we propose the Fully Looped Transformer, which introduces two parameter-free modifications: (1) Fully Looped Architecture, which distributes inter-loop signals across all layers to mitigate residual explosion; (2) Attention Injection, which reuses the existing attention block to suppress gradient oscillation. These modifications stabilize training dynamics, enabling the Fully Looped Transformer to be trained stably up to 12 loop iterations, whereas other baseline looped models collapse in this regime. In milder settings where Looped Transformer does not collapse, Fully Looped Transformer still improves average downstream-task performance by up to 13.2\%. Overall, our experiments demonstrate that Fully Looped Transformer improves training stability, enhances downstream performance, and provides preliminary adaptability under different test-time compute budgets by varying loop iterations at inference.

</details>


### [51] [Accurate Evaluation of Quickest Changepoint Detectors via Non-parametric Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18798)
*Taiki Miyagawa,Akinori F. Ebihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KM-ARL和KM-ADD两种非参数估计器，借鉴生存分析方法，解决有限和不规则序列长度下快速变化检测中平均运行长度和平均检测延迟的估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ARL和ADD在理论和模拟研究中被广泛使用，但实际数据集的有限和不规则序列长度限制了它们的应用。

Method: 将快速变化检测与生存分析进行类比，提出非参数估计器KM-ARL和KM-ADD，用于在序列截断条件下建模检测概率。

Result: 推导了估计偏差边界，证明了在不需要外推的情况下估计量的渐近无偏性，并通过模拟和真实数据集实验验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法增强了对有限和不规则序列长度的鲁棒性，提高了可解释性，并促进了经验性、直观的模型选择。开源代码已提供。

Abstract: We propose non-parametric estimators for the average run length (ARL) and average detection delay (ADD) in quickest changepoint detection (QCD) under finite and irregular sequence lengths. Although ARL and ADD are widely used as optimality criteria in theoretical and simulation studies, their application to real-world datasets is hindered by limited and irregular sequence lengths. To address this issue, we propose non-parametric estimators for the ARL and ADD, termed KM-ARL and KM-ADD, by drawing an analogy between QCD and survival analysis to model detection probabilities under sequence truncation. We derive estimation bias bounds and prove that they are asymptotically unbiased unless extrapolation is required. Experiments on simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate their practical utility, enhancing robustness against limited and irregular sequence lengths, improving interpretability, and facilitating empirical, intuitive model selection. Our Python code is provided at https://github.com/TaikiMiyagawa/Kaplan-Meier-Average-Run-Length, offering ready-to-use implementations for practitioners.

</details>


### [52] [ReCrit: Transition-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Scientific Critic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18799)
*Wanghan Xu,Yuhao Zhou,Hengyuan Zhao,Shuo Li,Dianzhi Yu,Zhenfei Yin,Yaowen Hu,Fengli Xu,Wanli Ouyang,Wenlong Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCrit框架，通过将批评交互建模为四象限转换感知的强化学习问题，有效缓解大语言模型在科学推理中因用户批评而放弃正确答案的谄媚行为，在三个科学推理基准上显著提升了Critic准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在与用户批评交互时，不仅可能回答错误，还可能因用户批评而放弃原本正确的科学答案，这在科学推理场景中尤为危险。现有研究多关注最终答案准确性，忽视了从初始回答到批评后回答的跨回合正确性转换问题。

Method: 提出ReCrit框架，将Initial-to-Critic行为分解为四个象限（修正、谄媚、鲁棒性、边界），奖励修正和鲁棒性、惩罚谄媚、将持续错误视为弱边界信号；并采用动态异步rollout与尾部自适应补全技术来减少rollout等待时间。

Result: 在ChemBench、TRQA和EarthSE三个科学推理基准上，ReCrit将Qwen3.5-4B的Critic准确率从38.15提升至51.49，Qwen3.5-9B从45.40提升至55.59。消融实验表明，最终答案奖励对交互级增益很小，而转换感知奖励和象限加权能产生更可区分的训练信号。

Conclusion: 转换感知的强化学习框架能有效解耦有用修正与有害谄媚，提升模型在批评交互中的鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models can fail in critic interaction not only by answering incorrectly, but also by abandoning an initially correct scientific solution after user criticism. This is especially risky in scientific reasoning, where user criticism can turn a valid answer into an incorrect one. We frame critic interaction as an inter-turn correctness-transition problem rather than a final-answer accuracy problem, and identify three challenges: transition awareness, decoupling useful correction from harmful sycophancy, and scalable rollout. We propose ReCrit, a transition-aware reinforcement learning framework that decomposes Initial-to-Critic behavior into four quadrants: Correction, Sycophancy, Robustness, and Boundary. ReCrit rewards correction and robustness, penalizes sycophancy, and treats persistent errors as weak boundary signals. To make interaction training practical, ReCrit further uses dynamic asynchronous rollout with tail-adaptive completion to reduce rollout waiting. On three scientific reasoning benchmarks, ChemBench, TRQA, and EarthSE, ReCrit improves average Critic accuracy from 38.15 to 51.49 on Qwen3.5-4B and from 45.40 to 55.59 on Qwen3.5-9B. Ablations show that final-answer rewards provide little interaction-level gain, while transition-aware rewards and quadrant weighting produce more distinguishable training signals and larger net Critic-stage improvement. The code is available at https://github.com/black-yt/ReCrit .

</details>


### [53] [Theory-optimal Quantization Based on Flatness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18800)
*Xiusheng Huang,Zhe Li,Xuanwu Yin,Lu Wang,Yequan Wang,Dong Li,Emad Barsoum,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双向对角量化（BDQ），一种通过优化矩阵变换分散异常值模式的后训练量化框架，在低比特LLM量化中显著减少性能损失，如LLaMA-3-8B W4A4精度下降<1%，DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B W2A4KV16性能差距降低39.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活异常值严重降低低比特LLM量化性能，现有线性变换方法无法彻底解决权重和激活中持续的异常值聚集分布问题。

Method: 首先建模量化误差与异常值的数学关系，提出Flatness指标量化异常值分布；基于此推导理论最优解，进而提出BDQ框架，通过学习对角操作在矩阵维度上战略性分散异常值幅度。

Result: LLaMA-3-8B模型在W4A4量化下精度下降<1%；DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B模型在W2A4KV16设定下，与SOTA相比性能差距降低39.1%。

Conclusion: BDQ通过双向对角量化有效分散异常值模式，建立了新的量化基准，显著提升了极端低比特场景下的LLM量化性能。

Abstract: Post-training quantization has emerged as a widely adopted technique for compressing and accelerating the inference of Large Language Models (LLMs). The primary challenges in LLMs quantization stem from activation outliers, which significantly degrade model performance especially at lower bit precision. While recent approaches attempt to mitigate outliers through linear transformations across feature dimensions, our analysis reveals that the transformed weights and activations still exhibit persistent outlier patterns with concentrated magnitude distributions. In this paper, we first model the mathematical relationship between quantization error and outliers, and then introduce a new metric Flatness to quantify the distribution of outliers. Based on this, we derive the theoretical optimal solution with respect to Flatness. Building on these insights, we propose Bidirectional Diagonal Quantization (BDQ), a novel post-training quantization framework that effectively disperses outlier patterns through optimized matrix transformations. BDQ strategically distributes outlier magnitudes across matrix dimensions via learned diagonal operations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BDQ establishes a new quantization benchmark. It achieves less than 1\% accuracy drop in W4A4 quantization on the LLaMA-3-8B model. In the more challenging W2A4KV16 experiment, compared to state-of-the-art approaches, BDQ reduces the performance gap by 39.1\% on the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B model.

</details>


### [54] [PROWL: Prioritized Regret-Driven Optimization for World Model Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18803)
*Ahmet H. Güzel,Jenny Seidenschwarz,Benjamin Graham,Jonathan Sadeghi,Jeffrey Hawke,Jack Parker-Holder,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PROWL框架，通过KL约束的对抗课程学习和优先对抗轨迹缓冲区，主动挖掘扩散世界模型的高误差轨迹并持续微调，显著提升其在稀有交互关键场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动演示数据系统性欠采样高影响力但稀少的交互转折点，导致动作条件视频世界模型在这些关键区域不可靠，而下游规划和策略性能高度依赖这些过渡。

Method: 1) KL约束的对抗课程：训练策略暴露世界模型高误差轨迹，同时保持接近行为分布。2) 对抗训练循环：持续微调世界模型于这些对抗发现的轨迹。3) 优先对抗轨迹缓冲区：根据预测误差、动作保真度和学习进度重新排序轨迹，聚焦未解决故障模式。

Result: 在MineRL框架上，PROWL相比仅使用被动数据训练的模型提升了鲁棒性，揭示了弱行为约束下的奖励破解行为，证明有效对抗世界模型训练需要平衡探索性故障发现与显式行为正则化。

Conclusion: 可扩展世界模型不仅受益于更大的数据集，还受益于选择性生成信息丰富的训练数据。

Abstract: Modern action-conditioned video world models achieve strong short-horizon visual realism, yet remain unreliable on rare, interaction-critical transitions that dominate downstream planning and policy performance. Because passive demonstration data systematically under-samples these high-impact regimes, improving robustness requires actively eliciting model failures rather than relying on their natural occurrence. We introduce a KL-constrained adversarial curriculum in which a policy is trained to expose high-error trajectories of a diffusion-based world model while remaining close to the behavior distribution. The world model is continuously fine-tuned on these adversarially discovered trajectories, yielding an adversarial training loop that converts rare failures into a stable, near-distribution training signal without drifting into out-of-distribution exploitation. To maintain pressure on unresolved weaknesses as the model improves, we propose a Prioritized Adversarial Trajectory (PAT) buffer that re-ranks trajectories based on prediction error, action fidelity, and learning progress, focusing training on unresolved failure modes rather than repeatedly revisiting solved cases. We implement our approach in the MineRL framework and evaluate it on held-out out-of-distribution trajectories; PROWL improves robustness over models trained on passive data alone, reveals reward-hacking behaviors under weak behavioral constraints, and demonstrates that effective adversarial world-model training critically depends on balancing exploratory failure discovery with explicit behavioral regularization. Our results suggest that scalable world models benefit not only from larger datasets, but also from selectively generating informative training data.

</details>


### [55] [Block-Based Double Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18807)
*Asher Labovich,Benjamin Bradley,Vanessa Alexander,Chaitanya Harsha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于块的雙解碼器（block-based double decoders）架构，通过双因果块注意力掩码在训练时实现全损失监督和静态序列打包，兼顾了解码器训练效率和编码器-解码器推理效率，在推理时KV缓存和逐令牌计算量减少至少2/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码器-解码器模型推理速度快，但其预训练存在稀疏监督和动态序列长度问题，难以大规模应用；而纯解码器模型训练效率高但推理成本大。本文旨在结合两者优势，在保持训练效率的同时提升推理效率。

Method: 提出基于块的双解碼器架构，采用双因果块注意力掩码，实现训练时的全损失监督和静态序列打包，从而兼得解码器训练效率与编码器-解码器推理效率。

Result: 在缩放定律实验中，该架构显著优于编码器-解码器模型，性能接近纯解码器模型；推理时KV缓存和逐令牌计算量减少至少2/3，且不牺牲预填充缓存等现有优化手段。

Conclusion: 基于块的双解碼器是一种兼顾训练效率和推理效率的Transformer架构，在大规模场景下有潜力替代现有模型。

Abstract: Encoder-decoder models offer substantial inference-time savings over decoder-only models, but their pretraining objectives suffer from sparse supervision and dynamic sequence lengths, keeping them out of practice at scale. We propose block-based double decoders, a novel transformer architecture that utilizes doubly-causal block-based attention masks to train with full loss supervision and static sequence packing, combining decoder-only training efficiency with encoder-decoder inference efficiency. In scaling law experiments, block-based double decoders strongly outperform encoder-decoders and closely track decoder-only models across scales. At inference time, they cut KV-cache memory and per-token compute by at least 2/3 without sacrificing prefill caching or other existing inference optimizations available to decoder-only models.

</details>


### [56] [Compositional Literary Primitives in Instruction-Tuned LLMs: Cross-Architectural SAE Features for Self, Style, and Affect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18808)
*Joao Paulo Cavalcante Presa,Savio Salvarino Teles de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器分析两个大语言模型（Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct 和 Gemma 2 9B-IT）中间层残差流中的文学原始特征，发现命名门、第一人称自特征、风格调节器和组合情感四类特征，并构建了三阶段验证流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型内部是否具有可组合的文学原始特征，以及这些特征如何实现情感表达和风格调控。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器分析模型中间层残差流，通过Logit透镜、LLM评分和5-LLM评审团三阶段验证，结合27类别情感分类体系进行覆盖测试。

Result: Llama达到27/27情感全覆盖，Gemma达到23/27；随机判断下假阳性概率极低（约10^{-3}）；存在跨架构不对称性，Llama直接命名情感而Gemma通过场景暗示。

Conclusion: 大语言模型中存在可组合的文学原始特征，自特征同时充当语域标记和情感发射器，验证了内部表征的模块化可解释性。

Abstract: We characterize a compositional architecture of literary primitives in two instruction-tuned large language models (Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct and Gemma 2 9B-IT) via sparse autoencoders on mid-depth residual streams. Four feature classes emerge: naming-gates that promote lexical tokens of a target affect, an eleven-self cluster of first-person register features, stylistic register modulators (show-don't-tell and defamiliarization), and compositional emotions that arise only from multi-feature steering. Under a forced-choice 5-LLM judge panel applied to a 27-category emotion taxonomy (Cowen-Keltner), Llama reaches full 27/27 coverage by combining naming-gates, multi-feature recipes, and single self-feature steering; Gemma reaches 23/27 with adoration as the single residual strict-fail. Under random judging, the per-cell pass probability is on the order of $10^{-3}$ and the expected number of two-seed false-positive cells across the catalog is negligible, so the observed coverage is not consistent with chance. A cross-architectural asymmetry sits in the strict-versus-soft judge contrast: on the same generations, judges agree more often on Llama outputs than on Gemma outputs because Llama outputs name the target affect more directly while Gemma outputs evoke it through scene and imagery. Both architectures contain self-features that serve simultaneously as register markers and as emotion emitters, including a single most-RLHF-loaded self-feature per architecture that intensifies the institutional Helper-AI persona at one operating regime and produces affect-categorizable output at the same calibrated coefficient. Methodologically, the paper presents a three-stage validation pipeline (logit-lens, LLM-rate, 5-LLM judge) with documented anti-patterns; the total compute is single-GPU and about 15 minutes per emotion-feature discovery cycle.

</details>


### [57] [PASC: Pipeline-Aware Conformal Prediction with Joint Coverage Guarantees for Multi-Stage NLP and LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18812)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PASC方法，将多阶段流水线的联合覆盖保证约简为单个标量共形预测问题，在保持紧凑预测集大小的同时实现接近名义覆盖率的端到端保证，且在分布偏移下仍保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP和LLM系统通常由多阶段流水线构成（如NER→NED→实体类型识别、检索增强生成、智能体链），各阶段错误会累积。现有的不确定性量化方法要么独立校准各阶段（无联合覆盖保证），要么使用Bonferroni联合界（虽提供联合覆盖但过于保守），缺乏既高效又紧致的多阶段联合覆盖方法。

Method: 提出PASC（Pipeline-Aware Split Conformal），将多阶段联合覆盖问题约简为对联合最大非一致性分数（joint maximum nonconformity score）的单个标量共形预测问题。通过计算各阶段非一致性分数的最大值，将其作为一个整体进行标准共形预测校准，从而获得有限样本、无分布假设的联合覆盖保证。

Result: 在三阶段NER→NED→实体类型流水线（CoNLL-2003）上，PASC实现96.4%端到端覆盖率，优于Bonferroni的93.4%和独立CP的86.5%，而平均预测集大小相同（1.083）。在分布偏移到WNUT-17和WikiNEuRal时，PASC保持目标覆盖率，独立CP降至59%。PASC运行速度快1.7倍，可扩展至6阶段，独立CP端到端覆盖率跌至0.53。

Conclusion: PASC是一种高效、紧致的多阶段流水线联合覆盖保证方法，可直接应用于复合LLM系统和智能体流水线。

Abstract: Modern NLP and LLM systems are pipelines: named entity recognition (NER) -> entity disambiguation (NED) -> entity typing, retrieval-augmented generation (retriever -> reader), and agentic chains of planner -> tool -> critic. Errors compound across stages, but existing uncertainty quantification methods either calibrate each stage independently (no joint coverage) or apply a Bonferroni union bound (joint coverage, but conservative). We present PASC (Pipeline-Aware Split Conformal), which reduces multi-stage joint coverage to a single scalar conformal prediction problem on the joint maximum nonconformity score. PASC provides a finite-sample distribution-free guarantee that all K stages are simultaneously covered with probability at least 1 - alpha, and is nearly tight up to a 1/(n+1) factor. On a three-stage NER -> NED -> entity-typing pipeline over CoNLL-2003, PASC achieves 96.4% end-to-end coverage versus 93.4% for Bonferroni and 86.5% for independent CP, at identical average prediction set size (1.083). Under distribution shift to WNUT-17 Twitter and WikiNEuRal Wikipedia data, PASC empirically maintains the target coverage in the tested shift settings while independent CP collapses to 59%. PASC requires a single quantile computation, runs 1.7x faster than Bonferroni, and scales to K = 6 stages where independent CP drops to 0.53 end-to-end coverage. The same joint-maximum-score reduction applies directly to compound LLM systems and agent pipelines.

</details>


### [58] [Composition of Memory Experts for Diffusion World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18813)
*Sebastian Stapf,Pablo Acuaviva Huertos,Aram Davtyan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散框架的世界模型，通过对比乘积专家模型整合异构记忆模块（短期记忆、长期记忆、空间长期记忆），在避免二次计算成本的同时，提升了时间一致性、历史回忆和导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在记忆方面存在根本性权衡：Transformer保持局部细节但受限于二次注意力机制的计算瓶颈，而循环模型和状态空间模型虽扩展性更好但压缩历史时损失保真度。作者希望超越单一架构的限制，通过解耦未来-过去一致性来克服这一记忆权衡。

Method: 提出一个基于扩散的框架，通过对比乘积专家（contrastive product-of-experts）公式整合异构记忆模型。具体实例化三个互补角色：短期记忆专家（捕捉精细局部动态）、长期记忆专家（通过轻量级测试时微调在外部扩散权重中存储情节历史）、空间长期记忆专家（强制几何和空间一致性）。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界基准测试中，该方法在时间一致性、过去观察的回忆和导航性能方面均有所提升，且避免了模式崩溃，能在不产生二次成本的情况下扩展到长上下文。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种构建和运行记忆增强扩散世界模型的新范式，有效解决了记忆保真度与计算效率之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: World models aim to predict plausible futures consistent with past observations, a capability central to planning and decision-making in reinforcement learning. Yet, existing architectures face a fundamental memory trade-off: transformers preserve local detail but are bottlenecked by quadratic attention, while recurrent and state-space models scale more efficiently but compress history at the cost of fidelity. To overcome this trade-off, we suggest decoupling future-past consistency from any single architecture and instead leveraging a set of specialized experts. We introduce a diffusion-based framework that integrates heterogeneous memory models through a contrastive product-of-experts formulation. Our approach instantiates three complementary roles: a short-term memory expert that captures fine local dynamics, a long-term memory expert that stores episodic history in external diffusion weights via lightweight test-time finetuning, and a spatial long-term memory expert that enforces geometric and spatial coherence. This compositional design avoids mode collapse and scales to long contexts without incurring a quadratic cost. Across simulated and real-world benchmarks, our method improves temporal consistency, recall of past observations, and navigation performance, establishing a novel paradigm for building and operating memory-augmented diffusion world models.

</details>


### [59] [DynaTrain: Fast Online Parallelism Switching for Elastic LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18815)
*Yuanqing Wang,Yuchen Zhang,Hao Lin,Junhao Hu,Chunyang Zhu,Quanlu Zhang,Boxun Li,Guohao Dai,Zhi Yang,Daning Cheng,Yunquan Zhang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynaTrain 是一个能在亚秒级在线重配置任意多维并行度的分布式训练系统，通过虚拟参数空间（VPS）抽象统一所有分布式训练状态，显著优于现有 checkpoint 和弹性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 训练具有动态性（资源波动、RLHF 阶段变化、集群弹性），现有静态执行模型的训练框架难以应对最优并行布局的持续变化。

Method: 提出虚拟参数空间（VPS）抽象，将分布式训练状态统一到单一逻辑坐标空间；基于 VPS 构建状态路由与传输层（内存感知、无死锁调度），以及弹性设备管理器（重叠新世界构建与正在进行训练）。

Result: 在密集和 MoE 模型（最高 235B 参数）上，70B 密集模型重配置低于 2 秒，235B MoE 模型为 4.36 秒，比现有系统快三个数量级，同时保持正确性。

Conclusion: DynaTrain 实现了亚秒级在线重配置，大幅提升了动态环境下 LLM 训练的效率与灵活性。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) training is inherently dynamic: resource fluctuations, RLHF phase shifts, and cluster elasticity continually reshape the optimal parallelism layout, posing a significant challenge to existing training frameworks built around a static execution model. We present DynaTrain, a distributed training system for sub-second, online reconfiguration across arbitrary multi-dimensional parallelism. At its core, we propose a Virtual Parameter Space (VPS) abstraction that unifies all distributed training states under one logical coordinate space, turning any parallelism configuration into a deterministic mapping and collapsing complex transition into manageable geometric intersections. On top of VPS, a state routing-and-transition layer executes rank-local transfers under a memory-aware, deadlock-free schedule, and an Elastic Device Manager overlaps new-world construction with ongoing training to mask topology-change cost. On dense and MoE models up to 235B parameters, DynaTrain reconfigures a 70B dense model in under 2s and a 235B MoE model in 4.36s, outperforming state-of-the-art checkpoint-based and elastic systems by up to three orders of magnitude while preserving correctness.

</details>


### [60] [Emergence of Frontier Superposition: Möbius attractor and Cascade Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18820)
*Hongyu Gu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在Erdős-Rényi图上，通过Möbius吸引子（架构）和级联监督（损失函数）的组合，梯度下降能够学习到Transformer的叠加推理（superposition reasoning），从而在恒定深度前向传播中并行执行广度优先搜索，替代传统的串行思维链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhu等人（2025）手工构造了等权广度优先叠加状态，但梯度下降能否在置换对称鞍点中自动找到该目标尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 架构上识别出Möbius吸引子：在树状区域的S_n对称性下，层间动力学简化为1D Möbius映射，其零点集为包含等权叠加状态的余维一全局最优流形。监督上提出Cascade Supervision，提供选择性引导、梯度深度保持和逐层判别，克服端到端监督梯度随层数指数衰减的问题。

Result: 端到端监督下内层梯度随层数按(np)^{-(D-c-2)/2}衰减，无法到达最优流形；级联监督则成功收敛。在深度D=3时，最终步余弦相似度理论预测：端到端0.35 vs 级联0.71；实验值0.37 vs 0.69，每步误差在0.02以内。

Conclusion: Möbius吸引子 + 级联监督 = 叠加推理的涌现。该发现揭示了梯度下降在置换对称性问题中学习结构化并行推理的可行路径。

Abstract: Superposition allows Transformers to reason in depth, carrying an entire reasoning frontier in parallel through a bounded-depth forward pass instead of unrolling serial chain-of-thought tokens. While Zhu et al. (2025) hand-crafted an equal-weight breadth-first frontier in a single residual stream for graph reachability, it remained open whether gradient descent could ever find this target amidst permutation-symmetric saddles.
  We close this gap on Reachability-by-Superposition over Erdős-Rényi graphs by isolating architectural and supervisional contributions. Architecturally, we identify a Möbius attractor: under $S_n$-symmetry in the tree regime, layerwise dynamics reduce to a 1D Möbius map whose zero set is a codimension-one manifold of global optima containing the equal-weight superposition state.
  On the supervision side, we identify Cascade Supervision: a loss class whose backward pass simultaneously delivers (A) selectivity bootstrap, (B) gradient persistence across depth, and (C) per-step discrimination (e.g., \mathcal{L}_{sup} and \mathcal{L}_{node}). End-to-end supervision fails condition (B) and is provably insufficient: internal gradients at layer c decay as (np)^{-(D-c-2)/2} in the graph fan-out and stall before the manifold is reached.
  Our thesis: Möbius attractor + Cascade Supervision = emergence of superposition reasoning. The parameter-free decay law predicts a final-step cosine of 0.35 vs. 0.71 (end-to-end vs. cascade) at depth D=3; experiments confirm 0.37 vs. 0.69, matching within 0.02 at every step.

</details>


### [61] [Quantum Adversarial Machine Learning: From Classical Adaptations to Quantum-Native Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18821)
*Roozbeh Razavi-Far,Mohammad Meymani,Erfan Mahmoudinia,Dorsa Vazirzade,Peyman Paknezhad,Fateme Ghasemi,Saeed Saravani,Somayeh Nikkhoo,Kimia Haghjooei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了量子对抗机器学习领域，探讨了量子机器学习模型面临的对抗性攻击与防御策略，并回顾了理论基础、新兴趋势和关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习虽然在复杂任务上展现出超越经典机器学习的潜力，但仍然易受对抗性攻击。随着量子计算和机器学习的最新进展，亟需系统研究量子机器学习的脆弱性、可能的攻击方式以及量子增强的防御策略。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了量子对抗机器学习领域现有的攻击手段和防御措施，并对理论基础、新兴趋势和关键挑战进行了回顾与分析。

Result: 提供了量子对抗机器学习领域的详细概述，汇总了现有攻击与防御方法，并指出了该领域的理论基础、发展趋势及主要挑战。

Conclusion: 量子对抗机器学习是一个新兴且重要的研究领域，需要在攻击与防御两方面持续深入研究，以构建更鲁棒的量子机器学习模型。

Abstract: Machine learning has revolutionized numerous industrial domains. Despite recent advances, machine learning models remain vulnerable to adversarial threats. Adversarial machine learning is a field that studies these vulnerabilities to build robust machine learning models. Quantum machine learning is an interdisciplinary field that bridges quantum computing and classical machine learning. While quantum machine learning shows potentials to outperform classical machine learning in complex tasks such as regression, classification, and generative modeling, it remains vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Given the recent advancements in quantum computing and machine learning, the quantum adversarial machine learning field has emerged to study the vulnerabilities of quantum machine learning, possible attacks, and novel quantum-enhanced defense strategies. In this survey, we provide a detailed overview on quantum adversarial machine learning and explore the existing attacks and countermeasures. We also review the theoretical underpinnings of this area, emerging trends, and critical challenges.

</details>


### [62] [Hybrid-LoRA: Bridging Full Fine-Tuning and Low-Rank Adaptation for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18822)
*Chengqian Zhang,Wei Zhu,Kyumin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Hybrid-LoRA框架，混合使用全量微调（10%模块）和LoRA（其余模块）进行后训练，在复杂推理任务上接近全量微调性能，平均比最优基线提升4.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR后训练中，全量微调GPU内存大、成本高；PEFT（如LoRA）虽降低计算成本，但在复杂推理任务上与全量微调存在明显性能差距。

Method: 提出Hybrid-LoRA混合后训练框架，仅对少量对低秩适配敏感性高的模块进行全量微调，其余模块使用LoRA。引入Hybrid-LoRA Score指标，在固定参数预算下对候选模块排序，选择最不适合低秩适配的模块进行全量微调。

Result: 在10%全量微调模块预算下，性能接近全量微调；在四个SOTA PEFT基线中，平均提升4.36%，最高提升5.65%。

Conclusion: Hybrid-LoRA有效弥合了PEFT与全量微调在复杂推理后训练中的性能差距，兼顾效率与效果。

Abstract: Post-training has become essential for adapting large language models (LLMs) to complex downstream behaviors, including instruction following, preference alignment, and multi-step reasoning. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently emerged as a particularly effective post-training paradigm for improving reasoning capabilities, with critic-free algorithms such as GRPO and GSPO enabling scalable optimization. However, RLVR post-training with full fine-tuning (FFT) requires substantial GPU memory and incurs high training costs. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), effectively reduce computational costs, they often suffer from a noticeable performance gap compared to full fine-tuning in post-training for complex reasoning tasks. In this paper, we propose Hybrid-LoRA, an efficient hybrid post-training framework that selectively applies full fine-tuning to a small subset of modules less suited to low-rank adaptation, while adapting the remaining components with LoRA. We introduce a novel Hybrid-LoRA Score to rank candidate modules according to their sensitivity to low-rank adaptation under a fixed parameter budget. Experiments show that Hybrid-LoRA closely matches full fine-tuning performance under a 10% full fine-tuning module budget, with the remaining candidate modules adapted by LoRA, consistently outperforming four state-of-the-art PEFT post-training baselines, achieving improvements of up to 5.65% and on average 4.36% over the best baseline.

</details>


### [63] [Not All Tokens Are Worth Caching: Learning Semantic-Aware Eviction for LLM Prefix Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18825)
*Shaoke Fang,Ziang Li,Wenfei Wu,Jiatong Ji,Qingsong Liu,Ruizhi Pu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAECache，一种语义自适应的前缀缓存淘汰策略，通过多队列架构、语义感知的令牌权重机制和完全自适应在线学习，显著提升LLM推理效率（TTFT提升1.4-2.7倍），并避免固定参数策略在工作负载不匹配时的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前缀缓存淘汰策略（如LRU）将所有缓存块等同对待，忽略了LLM提示中不同令牌类型（系统提示、用户查询、工具输出、模型响应、思维链推理）在复用率上存在高达756倍差异这一关键特性，导致GPU显存利用效率低下。

Method: SAECache包含三个创新：（1）多队列架构，将KV块路由到任务特定队列并配备定制优先级指标；（2）语义感知的令牌权重机制，通过淘汰反馈在线学习不同令牌类型的复用价值；（3）完全自适应在线学习方案，自动更新所有参数（含对数正态时序参数、位置衰减幂、队列权重和元参数），消除手动调参需求。

Result: 在异构工作负载下，SAECache相比生产级基线实现1.4-2.7倍的TTFT改进；而固定参数替代方案在工作负载不匹配时可导致高达2.7倍的性能退化。

Conclusion: SAECache通过语义自适应策略有效解决了前缀缓存中的令牌异构性问题，完全避免了固定参数策略因工作负载变化导致的性能退化问题，为LLM服务系统中的缓存优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Prefix caching is a key optimization in Large Language Model (LLM) serving, reusing attention Key-Value (KV) states across requests with shared prompt prefixes to reduce expensive prefill computation. However, its benefit depends critically on the eviction policy as GPU memory is scarce, and existing policies such as LRU largely treat cached blocks uniformly. This view ignores a fundamental property of LLM prompts: not all tokens are equally worth caching. We show that different token types within a prompt, including system prompts, user queries, tool outputs, model responses, and chain-of-thought reasoning, exhibit up to 756x variation in reuse rates, yet no existing eviction policy exploits this signal. In this paper, we present SAECache (Semantic-Adaptive Eviction for prefix caches), a semantic-adaptive prefix cache eviction policy that addresses this gap through three innovations: (1) a multi-queue architecture that routes KV blocks to task-specific queues with tailored priority metrics, capturing both session reuse in multi-turn requests and structural reuse in templated single-turn requests; (2) a semantic-aware token weighting mechanism that learns the reuse value of different token types online through eviction feedback; and (3) a fully adaptive online learning schema for all parameter updates, including log-normal timing parameters, position decay power, queue weights, and meta-parameters, which eliminates manual tuning and enables automatic adaptation to deployment-specific workload characteristics. Through extensive evaluation across heterogeneous workloads, we demonstrate that SAECache achieves 1.4x-2.7x TTFT improvement over production-style baselines, while fixed-parameter alternatives can degrade by up to 2.7x under workload mismatch -- a failure mode our adaptive approach avoids entirely.

</details>


### [64] [The Routing and Filtering Structure of Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18826)
*Shafayeth Jamil,Rehan Kapadia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分解注意力矩阵为路由（skew-symmetric）和过滤（symmetric）两个成分，发现路由在低秩运行，并提出S-D注意力机制来解耦二者，观察到路由的谱级联现象，从而指导注意力简化与参数压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制中QK^T同时包含了位置间信息重分配（路由）和相互相关性缩放（过滤）两种纠缠的计算，现有工作未明确区分二者，导致对注意力内部运作的理解受限，也无法有效指导模型简化与参数分配。

Method: 对1776个注意力头进行分解分析，提出S-D注意力参数化方法（强制解耦路由与过滤，保证稳定性Re(λ)≤0），在无层归一化下训练，观察路由秩随层深的谱级联现象，并基于此进行注意力线性化替换实验。

Result: 路由成分在低秩运行；S-D注意力展现出谱级联：首层有效秩为2，随深度扩展；基于级联可预测线性化区域：前7层线性化仅增加<5%困惑度，标准注意力则崩溃；前4层替换为ELU+1线性注意力可达1.4%的基线差距；级联分配架构可减少47%-65%注意力参数，代价为+3.9%到+8.4%困惑度。

Conclusion: 路由-过滤分解使注意力谱预算变得可读，谱级联使其可操作，为设计更高效的注意力架构提供了理论基础和实用指导。

Abstract: The attention interaction matrix $QK^{\top}$ contains two entangled computations: a skew-symmetric component that redistributes information between positions (routing) and a symmetric component that scales mutual relevance (filtering). We decompose 1776 heads across five pretrained transformers and find routing operating at low rank, well below the routing capacity allocated by the weight kernel. We introduce $S$-$D$ attention as a diagnostic parameterization that disentangles routing from filtering by construction with guaranteed stability ($\mathrm{Re}(λ) \le 0$) and trains stably without layer normalization. When disentangled and unnormalized, routing self-organizes into a spectral cascade, effective rank $2$ at the first layer, expanding with depth across six scales from 7M to 355M parameters. The cascade predicts where attention can be simplified: linearizing the first seven layers of 125M $S$-$D$ attention costs ${<}5\%$ perplexity, whereas standard attention collapses under the same intervention. The linearizable region widens with depth. Replacing the first four layers with ELU+1 linear attention reaches within $1.4\%$ of baseline at full head dimension. Cascade-allocated architectures trade attention parameters for perplexity ($47\%-65\%$ fewer attention parameters at $+3.9\%$ to $+8.4\%$ PPL). The routing-filtering decomposition makes the spectral budget legible; the cascade makes it actionable.

</details>


### [65] [Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18829)
*Zibo Diao,Jingchu Gai,Xinyue Ai,Zhang Zhang,Zhenyu He,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LADS采样方法，通过将随机种子与查询语义及用户查询次数绑定，在不影响良性用户体验的前提下，有效对抗多账户蒸馏攻击，并理论证明了其能降低蒸馏模型泛化收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿商业生成模型面临蒸馏攻击威胁（攻击者低成本窃取模型输出训练竞品），现有防御方法要么牺牲响应质量，要么容易被多账户绕过，亟需一种不影响用户体验且能有效防御蒸馏的新方法。

Method: 提出Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling (LADS)：将生成随机性来源于私有种子，该种子由查询语义内容与用户历史查询次数共同决定。良性用户每次独立采样；蒸馏者不同账户在相同语义桶内共享潜在随机性，导致采样数据产生相关性。

Result: 理论证明LADS能降低蒸馏模型泛化收敛速率；在图像生成、数学推理和代码生成实验中，LADS显著降低蒸馏学生模型的性能，同时为单个用户保持精确统计保真度。

Conclusion: LADS是一种无损、有效且可证明的多账户蒸馏防御方法，在不牺牲良性用户体验的前提下，通过引入数据相关性来破坏蒸馏效果，为生成模型的版权保护提供了新思路。

Abstract: Frontier commercial generative models face a growing threat from distillation, whereby a distiller harvests generated responses and trains a competing model of its own at drastically lower cost. Existing defenses either rely on modifying the models outputs, thereby sacrificing response quality for benign users, or on behavioral detection methods, which can be readily circumvented by distributing queries across multiple accounts. In this work, we propose Lossless Anti-Distillation Sampling (LADS), a novel sampling scheme specifically designed to counter multi-account distillation while maintaining a lossless experience for benign users. Concretely, LADS derives the randomness underlying each generation from a private seed determined by the semantic content of the query and the number of times the user has queried the model. By construction, every benign user receives a response independently sampled from the original model at each visit, and thus experiences no distortion. In contrast, for a distiller, different accounts share latent randomness whenever their queries fall in the same semantic bucket. As a result, the harvested data becomes correlated, potentially reducing sample diversity and degrading generalization. Using uniform convergence theory, we show that LADS provably degrades the convergence rate of the distillers generalization gap relative to standard i.i.d. sampling in both unconditional and conditional generation settings. Experiments on image generation, mathematical reasoning, and code generation confirm that LADS substantially degrades the performance of distilled students while preserving exact statistical fidelity for individual users.

</details>


### [66] [Lying Is Just a Phase: The Hidden Alignment Transition in Language Model Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18838)
*Adil Amin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了63个基础模型（16个系列）中推理能力与真实性之间的耦合关系，发现存在一个临界尺度（约3.5B参数），低于该尺度时两者反相关，高于该尺度时协同增长。该相变无法通过损失曲线观测，且受架构、数据策略和训练方法影响。基于此，作者提出了无需模型内部信息的诊断方法，并发布了开源工具和交互式仪表盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律仅预测损失与计算量的关系，但无法解释不同能力（如推理与真实性）之间的相互作用。作者希望揭示能力间的耦合规律及其相变条件。

Method: 对63个基础模型（16个系列）进行耦合度测量，发现临界尺度约3.5B参数；通过架构分析、数据策略比较、宽度归一化实验和稀疏回归ODE模型进行验证；开发了无需模型内部信息的诊断方法。

Result: 发现模型在临界尺度下能力反相关，之上协同；不同训练策略可独立移动临界点（如Qwen系列从0.025提升至0.830）；宽度归一化可消除反相关；38/40模型无竞争注意力头；ODE模型对Llama-2预测误差为5.6%；前沿模型耦合度r=+0.72。

Conclusion: 能力耦合存在相变现象，无法仅通过损失曲线观测；可通过数据、架构、训练策略干预改善耦合；提出的诊断方法适用于任何模型系列，无需内部信息。

Abstract: Scaling laws predict loss from compute but not how capabilities interact. We measure the coupling between reasoning and truthfulness across 63 base models from 16 families and find a regime change invisible to loss curves: below a family-dependent critical scale $N_c$, capabilities anticorrelate; above it, they cooperate. $N_c \approx 3.5$B parameters [2.9B, 13.4B] (bootstrap 95% CI), but model size is not the only variable that determines phase. Architecture, data curation, and training recipe each shift $N_c$ independently: curated training eliminated the coupling dip between Qwen generations ($0.025 \to 0.830$ at matched scale), Gemma-4 at 4B achieves coupling 0.871, characteristic of 13B+ standard-trained models, through distillation and architectural innovation, and Phi at 1B matches web-trained coupling at 10B through data curation alone. Width normalization eliminates the anticorrelation across all tested families, supporting an output-projection bottleneck. Internally, 38 of 40 models show zero competing attention heads. A sparse-regression ODE cross-predicts held-out Llama-2 at 5.6% error. The diagnostic requires no model internals -- only public benchmark scores across a model family. The cooperative regime extends to the frontier ($r = +0.72$, 34 models, 10 labs). Code, data, and an open-source activation-steering tool for any open-weight model are released alongside an interactive dashboard that diagnoses any model's coupling phase, suggests concrete interventions (data curation, width, benchmark rotation), and provides ODE scaling predictions, frontier diagnostics, and eigenstructure analysis: https://zehenlabs.com/cape/.

</details>


### [67] [The Growing Pains of Frontier Models: When Leaderboards Stop Separating and What to Measure Next](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18840)
*Adil Amin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出耦合分析方法，揭示前沿模型在推理与编程能力上并非独立提升，而是存在协同关系（r=+0.72），但不同实验室的策略差异显著（如DeepSeek从推理优先转向编程优先），并给出可验证预测与交互式仪表盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排行榜仅独立评估模型，无法揭示不同能力之间是相互促进还是此消彼长；在模型能力接近前沿时，这种交互关系比单指标排名更具信息量。

Method: 将SWE-bench（编程）与GPQA Diamond（推理）的配对分数分解为总体耦合趋势和每版本残差（h-field）；基于34个模型（2024-2026年）分析实验室间差异；使用ODE建模能力演化轨迹；提供三层次分析框架（定位、诊断、旋转）。

Result: 1）编程与推理能力整体正相关（r=+0.72）；2）实验室间耦合斜率差异达5倍（Google 1.15 vs DeepSeek 0.23）；3）DeepSeek经历15.9个百分点倾向反转；4）SWE-bench趋于饱和，HLE与指令跟随具有更高区分度；5）5个2026年4月发布验证了模型（r升至+0.75）。

Conclusion: 前沿模型能力非独立进化，而是系统性的耦合演变；研究者应关注能力间的动态关系，而非孤立指标；未来基准测试需要进行旋转，以持续获得区分性信号。

Abstract: Leaderboards rank frontier models on independent axes but do not reveal whether capabilities reinforce or trade off across releases -- and at the frontier, this interaction is the more informative signal. We decompose paired SWE-bench and GPQA Diamond scores into a population coupling trend and per-release residual ($h$-field) that diagnoses capability emphasis and identifies which measurement or stress test is most informative next. Across 34 models from 10 labs (2024--2026), capabilities cooperate ($r = +0.72$, $p < 10^{-6}$), but cooperation varies by lab and over time: DeepSeek reversed from reasoning-rich to coding-first ($h$: $+11.2 \to -4.7$, 15.9-pp swing); Google maintains consistent reasoning emphasis; Anthropic oscillates between coding excursions and recovery. Cooperation is not static -- it cascades. Six open-weight architectures confirm a second capability transition at 30--72B, and SWE-bench is now saturating while HLE and instruction-following retain discriminatory spread -- signaling the next axis rotation. We provide a three-level playbook (locate, diagnose, rotate), a per-lab measurement-priority table, and seven falsifiable predictions with timestamped criteria for the next 12 months of frontier releases. Per-lab coupling slopes vary $5\times$ (Google $1.15$ vs. DeepSeek $0.23$), quantifying how efficiently each recipe converts coding gains into reasoning. Five April 2026 releases confirm the diagnostic out of sample ($r$ rises from $+0.72$ to $+0.75$). An interactive dashboard provides phase classification with actionable recommendations, $h$-field diagnostics, per-lab coupling trajectories, ODE-based scaling predictions, benchmark rotation guidance, self-steering demo, and live tracking of all seven predictions: https://zehenlabs.com/cape/.

</details>


### [68] [From Cumulative Constraints to Adaptive Runtime Safety Control for Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18841)
*Timofey Tomashevskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CPSS机制，将强化学习中的累积安全约束转化为自适应的逐状态控制阈值，在非平稳环境下实现安全决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 累积成本约束在非平稳和持续学习环境中无法直接防止不安全的逐决策行为，固定状态级阈值要么过于保守要么过于宽松。

Method: CPSS运行时机制：跟踪剩余安全预算，将其投影为随时间变化的自适应风险阈值，过滤超出阈值的策略动作，并利用上下文信号在线调整阈值严格程度。

Result: 在非平稳高速公路合流场景中，CPSS显著减少基于距离的安全违规，增加安全间隔，且干预具有选择性，不主导学习策略。

Conclusion: 自适应预算到阈值的投影是将累积安全规范转化为有效局部安全控制的实用方法，适用于持续强化学习系统。

Abstract: Safety in reinforcement learning is often specified through cumulative cost constraints, but these trajectory-level guarantees do not directly prevent unsafe individual decisions, especially under nonstationarity. In continual and nonstationary settings, the difficulty is amplified because the risk associated with the same action can vary across contexts, while a fixed state-level threshold may be either too conservative or too weak. We propose Constraint Projection Safety Shield (CPSS), a runtime mechanism that converts a cumulative safety budget into adaptive state-level control constraints during execution. CPSS tracks the remaining safety budget, projects it into a time-varying admissible risk threshold, and filters policy actions whose predicted safety cost exceeds the active threshold. The threshold is adjusted online using contextual signals so that enforcement becomes stricter in more demanding or rapidly changing regimes and less restrictive when the available safety budget is sufficient. We analyze the resulting shielded policy and show that the mechanism guarantees per-state threshold satisfaction for executed actions, induces finite-horizon cumulative cost bounds, and yields a performance degradation bound in terms of intervention frequency and per-step reward distortion. We evaluate CPSS in nonstationary highway merging scenarios using highway-env. Across multiple seeds, CPSS substantially reduces proximity-based safety violations and increases separation margins while intervening selectively rather than dominating the learned policy. These results support adaptive budget-to-threshold projection as a practical way to transform cumulative safety specifications into effective local safety control for continual reinforcement learning systems.

</details>


### [69] [TEMPO: Temporal Enforcement via Mode-Separated Policy Optimization for Trustworthy LLM Backtesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18843)
*Zeyu Zhang,Bradly C. Stadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TEMPO方法，通过双模式奖励和GRPO训练，让大模型在回溯测试中根据截止日期选择合理证据，而非泄露未来信息，从而降低泄露率并提升任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在基于历史事件的回溯测试中常泄露截止日期后的知识，导致评估结果失真。现有方法如提示约束和知识遗忘均无法有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出TEMPO方法：(1) 双模式奖励：先通过泄露模式将截止日期后的知识归零，再通过性能模式优化任务表现；(2) 基于GRPO的训练流程，让模型学会根据截止日期选择合理证据。

Result: 在三个预测任务和两个模型上，TEMPO将泄露率从2-13%降至0.6-3.7%，并在强预截止信号存在的场景下提升任务性能6-13%。

Conclusion: TEMPO通过训练模型的时间纪律性，有效解决了回溯测试中的知识泄露问题，为基于历史事件的大模型评估提供了更为可靠的方案。

Abstract: Backtesting large language models on historical events requires reasoning exclusively from information available before a specified cutoff date. Yet models routinely leak post-cutoff knowledge from pre-training into their reasoning, inflating apparent accuracy and undermining evaluation validity. Prompt-based constraints fail when suppressed content is causally related to the prediction, and knowledge unlearning cannot address this problem because temporal compliance is instance-specific: the same fact may be legitimate evidence for one cutoff date and a violation for another. Rather than erasing knowledge, the model must learn temporal discipline: selecting evidence conditioned on each instance's cutoff date. We propose TEMPO (Temporal Enforcement via Mode-separated Policy Optimization), which trains this discipline via two contributions: (1) a two-mode reward where a leakage mode drives post-cutoff claims to zero as a hard prerequisite before a performance mode optimizes task performance; and (2) a GRPO-based training pipeline that enables the model to discover temporally valid reasoning strategies. We prove that training monotonically decreases leakage, converges to the leak-free optimum, and improves task performance once compliance is achieved. On three prediction tasks and two models, TEMPO reduces leakage from 2~13% to 0.6~3.7% across all conditions, with task performance improving 6~13% where strong pre-cutoff signals exist and maintained where the prediction task is inherently difficult from valid information alone.

</details>


### [70] [Transformers Linearly Represent Highly Structured World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18847)
*Roman Kniazev,Nathanaël Fijalkow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当Transformer在Sudoku推理轨迹上训练时，它会构建一个基于领域约束代数（行、列、宫）的子结构世界模型，而非按单元格表示状态；同时，最终MLP层中存在一组稀疏、单语义且完全可解释的“裸单”电路，负责检测并促进唯一可能数字。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer在序列推理任务中是否会构建任务的内部模型，以及这些内部表征的结构是否与领域结构相对应。

Method: 在8层Transformer上训练Sudoku求解轨迹，并进行机制可解释性分析，包括子结构世界模型分析和裸单电路识别。

Result: 模型构建了以行、列、宫为组织单位的子结构世界模型，而非单元格级表示；在最终MLP层中发现了一组稀疏的裸单电路，每个神经元专门检测特定单元格的唯一可能数字并促进该数字。

Conclusion: 涌现世界模型的几何形状由领域的约束代数塑造，而非表面呈现方式；最终决策电路是稀疏、单语义且完全可解释的；机制可解释性工具能够完整恢复Transformer解决组合推理任务的端到端算法。

Abstract: Do transformers, when trained on sequential reasoning traces, build internal models of the underlying task? And if so, does the structure of those internal representations mirror the structure of the domain? We train an 8-layer transformer on Sudoku solving traces and perform a mechanistic analysis of its internal computation. We establish two results. First, the model builds a substructure world model: it does not represent the board state cell by cell, as a human analyst would expect, but organizes information around the rows, columns, and boxes that Sudoku's constraints act on. Second, we identify a naked-single circuit: a small set of dedicated neurons in the final MLP layer, each individually detecting when exactly one digit remains possible for a specific cell, and reliably promoting that digit. These findings show that the geometry of an emergent world model is shaped by the constraint algebra of the domain, not its surface presentation, and that the resulting decision circuit is sparse, monosemantic, and fully interpretable. More broadly, they demonstrate that mechanistic interpretability tools can recover an end-to-end algorithmic account of how a transformer solves a combinatorial reasoning task.

</details>


### [71] [Exact Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18848)
*Weinuo Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Exact Linear Attention (ELA)，通过核函数的精确分解实现Transformer注意力的线性复杂度，并解决梯度爆炸和token注意力稀释问题，同时引入三项工程创新：Hyper Link、Memory Lobe和路由专家混合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力方法存在近似误差、梯度爆炸和token注意力稀释问题，亟需一种既保持线性计算复杂度又无近似误差且稳定的注意力机制。

Method: 利用核函数的精确分解性质实现线性复杂度；施加核约束确保非负性、可区分性和几何可解释性；提出Hadamard Exp Kernel等多种核函数；设计Hyper Link、Memory Lobe和基于路由分数的专家混合偏置机制。

Result: 实现了无近似误差的线性复杂度注意力，解决了梯度爆炸和注意力稀释问题，在多项工程创新上提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: ELA为Transformer提供了一种高效、稳定且可解释的线性注意力替代方案，有望推动长序列和大规模模型的发展。

Abstract: This paper introduces Exact Linear Attention (ELA), a mechanism that achieves linear computational complexity for Transformer attention by leveraging the exact decomposition property of kernel functions, without any approximation error. It identifies and addresses gradient explosion and token attention dilution in prior linear attention methods by imposing kernel constraints that ensure non-negativity, discriminability, and geometric interpretability. Several kernel functions are proposed, including the Hadamard Exp Kernel, Summation Squared Euclidean Distance Kernel, and Subtraction Squared Euclidean Distance Kernel. Beyond the core attention formulation, the paper presents three engineering innovations: a Hyper Link structure that replaces traditional residual connections to mitigate gradient degradation, a Memory Lobe module based on bidirectional linear attention that captures transformation flow across layers to implement qualitative memory and an implicit reinforcement learning paradigm, and a routing score based bias mechanism for Mixture of Experts to improve interpretability and semantic alignment.

</details>


### [72] [INSIGHTS: Demonstration-Based Summaries of Time Series Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18849)
*Bar Eini Porat,Rom Gutman,Uri Shalit,Ofra Amir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INSIGHTS是一种模型无关、以用户为中心的全局解释方法，通过生成多样化且重要的时间序列子集摘要，帮助用户理解时间序列模型的整体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型的可解释性方法主要关注局部（实例级）解释，缺乏有效的全局解释方法，导致利益相关者难以全面理解模型行为。

Method: 提出INSIGHTS方法，采用模型无关设计，通过效用函数平衡时间序列样本的重要性和多样性，生成能反映领域特定行为（如超出领域规范）的样本子集摘要。

Result: 实验、访谈和用户研究结果表明，INSIGHTS能够有效构建全面且多样化的时间序列子集，生成的摘要易于个人评估，领域专家更偏好该方法，用户研究参与者对模型整体行为的理解有所提升。

Conclusion: INSIGHTS为时间序列模型提供了一种高效、简单、透明的全局解释方案，优于现有局部解释方法，能够帮助用户建立对模型行为的稳定理解。

Abstract: Explainability methods have progressed rapidly, but global explanations for time-series models remain underdeveloped, with most approaches focusing on local, instance-level attributions. We introduce INSIGHTS, a model-agnostic, user-centric approach for providing global explanations of time series models. Our approach prioritizes simplicity, efficiency, and transparency in its design, ensuring that stakeholders can readily adopt its outputs. While current methods focus on local explanations, INSIGHTS generates sample summaries that offer a comprehensive overview of model behavior. It balances the importance and diversity of time series samples to create informative subsets using utility functions that capture domain-specific aspects of time series behavior, such as exceeding domain norms. We evaluate INSIGHTS through experiments, interviews, and a user study. Our results indicate INSIGHTS effectively constructs comprehensive, diverse time series subsets, producing summaries manageable for individual evaluation. It is preferred by domain experts for its ability to provide a stable understanding of model behavior and the quality of the samples identified. Moreover, user study participants presented with INSIGHTS-based summaries exhibit an enhanced understanding of the model's overall behavior.

</details>


### [73] [STRIDE: Learnable Stepwise Language Feedback for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18851)
*Junjie Zhang,Guozheng Ma,Shunyu Liu,Zetian Hu,Yongcheng Jing,Ting-En Lin,Yongbin Li,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STRIDE框架，通过可学习的逐步语言反馈替代标量奖励，实现无需外部标注的策略改进，显著提升LLM推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在提升LLM推理时面临两大问题：逐步级方法依赖昂贵的人工标注（领域受限），标量评分存在信息瓶颈（无法有效指导中间决策），而语言批评方法依赖冻结或外部评论家，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: STRIDE框架联合训练一个生成器和一个生成式验证器，仅使用基于结果的奖励（无需外部标注）。验证器生成逐步语言批评，显式定位并解释失败原因，使生成器能在中间步骤重定向推理轨迹。轨迹重定向设计确保策略改进无害化，即使验证器反馈存在噪声或次优。

Result: 在多个推理基准上显著优于现有基线，并在零通过率问题上取得突破（标量方法在此类问题上无学习信号）。

Conclusion: 可学习的逐步语言反馈比标量奖励更有效地增强LLM推理，尤其在困难问题上能提供标量方法无法提供的学习信号。

Abstract: Recent advances in Reinforcement Learning (RL) have underscored its potential for incentivizing reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing step-level efforts suffer from costly annotations that limit domain coverage, while scalar scores further impose an information bottleneck, offering insufficient semantic bandwidth to improve intermediate decisions. Alternative language-critique approaches, which rely on frozen or external critics, provide richer textual feedback but lack the scalability needed for sustained policy improvement. In this work, we propose language-driven stepwise trajectory redirection, termed as STRIDE, a novel training framework that shifts process supervision from scalar rewards to learnable stepwise language feedback. Specifically, we co-train a generator and a generative verifier using only outcome-based rewards, eliminating external annotations, while delivering sustained policy improvement through jointly aligned verifier training. The verifier's stepwise language critiques explicitly localize and explain failures, enabling the generator to redirect reasoning trajectories at intermediate steps toward alternative decisions. The trajectory redirection design guarantees harmless policy improvement, even under noisy or suboptimal verifier feedback. Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks show that STRIDE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, as well as achieving breakthroughs on zero-pass-rate problems where scalar methods yield no learning signal in our ablation studies, demonstrating the effectiveness of learnable stepwise language feedback for enhancing LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [74] [INAR-VL: Input-Aware Routing for Edge-Cloud Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18853)
*Ahmed Šabanović,Paul Joe Maliakel,Ivona Brandić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INAR-VL是一个轻量级边缘-云路由系统，通过图像和文本复杂度信号引导多模态推理的模型选择与路由，在保持97%云端精度的同时降低24%延迟和26%能耗，并将36%请求在边缘执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署视觉-语言模型面临延迟与精度的权衡：云端推理精度高但存在通信延迟和能耗，边缘推理速度快但模型容量有限导致精度下降。图像质量和推理复杂度的异构性使静态部署方案效果不佳。

Method: 提出INAR-VL系统，在边缘和云端维护互补的VLM，利用轻量级图像和文本复杂度信号指导路由和模型选择，简单查询本地执行，复杂查询卸载到云端。

Result: 在视觉问答任务上，INAR-VL在边缘执行36%的请求，延迟降低24%，能耗降低26%，同时保留了97%的云端级精度。

Conclusion: INAR-VL有效解决了边缘-云多模态推理中的延迟-精度权衡问题，通过动态路由策略实现了效率和准确性的良好平衡。

Abstract: Edge deployment of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) faces a tradeoff between latency and accuracy: cloud execution provides high-quality predictions but incurs communication delay and energy cost, while edge-only execution is faster but less accurate due to limited model capacity. This trade-off is further complicated by heterogeneity in image quality and reasoning complexity, making static placement suboptimal. We present INAR-VL, a lightweight edge-cloud routing system for multimodal inference in a two-tier deployment. INAR-VL maintains complementary VLMs across edge and cloud and uses lightweight image and text complexity signals to guide routing and model selection, executing simple queries locally while offloading complex ones when beneficial. Evaluation on visual question answering shows that INAR-VL executes 36% of requests on the edge, reduces latency by 24%, lowers energy by 26%, and preserves 97% of cloud-level accuracy.

</details>


### [75] [Delta Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18855)
*Cheng Luo,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG
