TL;DR: 本文提出Delta Attention Residuals，通过关注层间变化量(delta)而非累积状态来解决注意力残差中的路由崩溃问题，在220M-7.6B规模上取得1.7-8.2%的验证困惑度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Attention Residuals关注累积隐藏状态导致深层路由崩溃（注意力权重趋近均匀分布，最大权重仅约0.2），限制了模型选择信息性状态的能力。

Method: 提出Delta Attention Residuals，将注意力机制应用于每层子层的变化量（delta = h_{i+1} - h_i）而非累积状态，可在子层和块粒度应用。

Result: 在220M-7.6B规模上，Delta Attention Residuals持续优于标准残差和Attention Residuals，获得1.7-8.2%的验证困惑度增益，且可通过微调将预训练检查点转换。

Conclusion: Delta表示具有结构多样性，能产生更高对比度的注意力分布（最大权重约0.6），实现更选择性和高效的跨层路由。

Abstract: Attention Residuals replace standard additive residual connections with learned softmax attention over previous layer outputs, enabling selective cross-layer routing. However, standard Attention Residuals still attend over cumulative hidden states in previous layers, which are highly redundant. We show that this redundancy leads to routing collapse in deeper layers: attention weights become low-contrast and closer to uniform (max weight ${\approx}$0.2), limiting the model's ability to select informative states in previous layers. This raises a key but underexplored design question: what layer-wise representations should be routed in Attention Residuals? To answer this question, we propose Delta Attention Residuals, which attend over deltas -- the change introduced by each sublayer ($\mathbf{v}_i = \mathbf{h}_{i+1} - \mathbf{h}_i$) -- instead of cumulative states. Delta representations are structurally diverse and yield higher-contrast attention distributions (max weight ${\approx}$0.6), enabling more selective and effective routing across layers. This principle applies at both per-sublayer and block granularity. Across all tested scales (220M--7.6B), Delta Attention Residuals consistently outperform both standard residuals and Attention Residuals, with 1.7--8.2\% validation perplexity gains. Delta Attention Residuals also enables converting pretrained checkpoints into Delta Attention Residuals via standard fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/wdlctc/delta-attention-residuals-code.

</details>


### [76] [SPHERICAL KV: Angle-Domain Attention and Rate-Distortion Retention for Efficient Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18856)
*Anay Chauhan,Gurucharan Marthi Krishna Kumar,Arion Das,Amit Dhanda,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Spherical KV 提出将 KV 缓存分配建模为率失真问题，通过角度域注意力（ADA）和率失真保留（RDR）两个组件，在长上下文推理中同时降低 KV 驻留内存并保持解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中 KV 缓存的内存占用随上下文长度线性增长，且解码过程受限于 HBM 流式传输而非计算，现有方法（逐出、窗化、量化、卸载）虽减少内存占用，但未有效解决解码关键路径上的瓶颈，尤其是压缩状态仍需解码为密集向量。

Method: 提出 Spherical KV 方法，包含两个核心组件：(i) 角度域注意力（ADA），将键存储为球面参数化（标量半径 + 紧凑角度码），直接基于角度码计算注意力 logits，避免重建密集键；(ii) 率失真保留（RDR），在固定预算下联合选择每个 token 和 head 的保留/丢弃决策及精度层级，生成层级同构页面以支持轻量元数据和合并读取。

Result: ADA 保持分页、块局部、融合友好的解码路径，直接减少实际服务场景中的 HBM 流量；RDR 在固定预算下实现层级同构页面，通过轻量元数据和合并读取提升解码效率。两者共同降低 KV 驻留内存并保持解码效率。

Conclusion: Spherical KV 将 KV 分配建模为率失真问题，借助注意力几何特性，为部署导向的长上下文推理提供了一种减少 KV 内存占用同时保持解码效率的有效机制。

Abstract: Long-context inference is increasingly constrained by the KV cache: resident memory grows with context length, and decoding becomes limited by repeated High Bandwidth Memory (HBM) streaming rather than arithmetic. Existing methods such as eviction, windowing, quantization, and offloading reduce footprint, but often leave the critical-path bottleneck only partially addressed, especially when compressed states must still be reconstructed into dense vectors during decoding.
  We present Spherical KV, a long-context inference method that treats KV allocation as a rate-distortion problem grounded in attention geometry for efficient decoding. The method is built on two ideas: (i) represent directional information cheaply in the decode hot loop, and (ii) allocate retention and precision according to estimated future utility. Its first component, Angle-Domain Attention (ADA), stores keys in a spherical parameterization consisting of a scalar radius and compact angle codes, and computes attention logits directly from these codes without reconstructing dense keys. This preserves a paged, block-local, fusion-friendly decode path and directly targets HBM traffic in realistic serving settings. Its second component, Rate-Distortion Retention (RDR), jointly chooses keep/drop decisions and precision tiers per token and head under a fixed budget, producing tier-homogeneous pages with lightweight metadata and coalesced reads. Together, ADA and RDR provide a deployment-oriented mechanism for reducing KV residency while preserving decode efficiency.

</details>


### [77] [Towards Family-Grouped Hierarchical Federated Learning on Sub-5KB Models: A Feasibility Study of Privacy-Preserving ECG Monitoring for Ultra-Resource-Constrained Wearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18862)
*Hangyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Family-FL，一种基于家庭隐私边界的三层联邦学习架构，结合仅669参数、INT8量化后占用4.65KB Flash和2.95KB RAM的Tiny CNN-LSTM模型，在MIT-BIH数据集上实现了比FedAvg减少76.7%通信量、91.9%准确率的心律失常检测，为超低功耗微控制器上的隐私保护联邦学习提供了技术可行性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病是全球首要死因，可穿戴设备上的持续ECG监测有助于早期检测心律失常，但标准联邦学习通信开销大，且深度学习模型无法在超低功耗微控制器上部署，亟需一种兼顾隐私、通信效率和硬件约束的解决方案。

Method: 提出Family-Grouped Hierarchical Federated Learning (Family-FL)三层架构，以家庭作为自然隐私边界进行族内聚合后再全局同步；设计硬件约束下的Tiny CNN-LSTM模型（669参数），经INT8量化后仅需4.65KB Flash和2.95KB RAM，适配STC32G12K128类微控制器。

Result: 在MIT-BIH心律失常数据库上（5次独立运行平均）：Family-FL相比FedAvg通信量减少76.7%，准确率相当；Family-FL-Tiny达到91.9±1.2%准确率，macro-F1为0.483±0.031，总通信量仅为FedAvg的0.31%；室性心律失常检测F1=0.80。

Conclusion: 通过仿真评估证明了在超资源受限微控制器上实现隐私保护联邦学习的技术可行性。但存在局限性：未进行硬件部署、仅使用单数据集（47名受试者）、罕见类别敏感度较低、缺乏正式差分隐私保证。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease remains the leading cause of death worldwide, and early detection of arrhythmias through continuous ECG monitoring on wearable devices can prevent life-threatening events. Federated Learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving collaborative training by keeping raw ECG data on device, yet standard FL incurs prohibitive communication overhead and standard deep learning models cannot fit on ultra-low-power microcontrollers. We propose Family-Grouped Hierarchical Federated Learning (Family-FL), a three-tier architecture that uses the family as a natural privacy boundary for intra-family aggregation before global synchronization. We further design a hardware-constrained Tiny CNN-LSTM architecture with only 669 parameters, INT8-quantized to occupy merely 4.65KB Flash and 2.95KB RAM, meeting the constraints of STC32G12K128-class microcontrollers. Experiments on the MIT-BIH Arrhythmia Database (mean of 5 independent runs with different seeds) demonstrate that Family-FL reduces communication volume by 76.7% compared to FedAvg while maintaining comparable accuracy. Family-FL-Tiny achieves 91.9 +/- 1.2% accuracy with macro-F1 of 0.483 +/- 0.031, reducing total communication to 0.31% of FedAvg. The model achieves reliable ventricular arrhythmia detection (per-class F1 = 0.80), the most clinically critical abnormality for home-based preliminary screening. These results demonstrate the technical feasibility of privacy-preserving federated learning on ultra-resource-constrained microcontrollers through simulation-based evaluation. We honestly discuss limitations: no hardware deployment, single-dataset validation (MIT-BIH, 47 subjects), reduced rare-class sensitivity, and absence of formal differential privacy guarantees.

</details>


### [78] [SAGE: Shaping Anchors for Guided Exploration in RLVR of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18864)
*Chanuk Lee,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出RLVR在提升pass@1时有效，但pass@k提升不足，原因是反向KL正则化锚定策略于参考分布，抑制了推理模式多样性。提出SAGE框架，通过引导函数重塑反向KL的锚定分布，实现可控经验支持扩展，在数学推理基准上同时提升pass@1和pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在推理任务上提升pass@1效果显著，但pass@k提升有限，引发其是否真正让LLM获得新推理能力的疑问。先前分析认为标准RLVR目标的结构特性导致探索压力不足，本文旨在揭示这一结构性约束并寻求解决方案。

Method: 提出SAGE框架，通过引导函数q(x,y)重塑反向KL正则化的锚定分布，实现可控的经验支持扩展，从而在不破坏效率-覆盖权衡的前提下提升推理模式的多样性。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数学推理基准上，SAGE在pass@1和pass@k指标上均取得了一致的改进效果。

Conclusion: 反向KL正则化是限制RLVR探索多样推理模式的关键结构约束，简单地移除或替换为前向KL均不可行；SAGE通过重塑锚定分布有效缓解了这一问题，实现了更好的效率-覆盖权衡。

Abstract: Recent studies observe that reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) reliably improves pass@1 on reasoning tasks, yet often fails to yield comparable gains in pass@k, raising the question of whether RLVR genuinely enables large language models to acquire novel reasoning abilities or merely enhances the efficiency of sampling reasoning modes already present in the base model. Prior analyses largely support the latter view, attributing this limitation to structural properties of standard RLVR objectives that result in insufficient exploration pressure. In this work, we argue that a central structural constraint arises from reverse-KL regularization, which stabilizes training but inherently anchors the policy to the reference distribution, thereby suppressing the emergence of alternative reasoning modes. However, we show that neither removing the KL term nor replacing it with forward-KL provides a satisfactory solution, as both disrupt the efficiency-coverage trade-off by either inducing reward hacking or allocating probability mass to off-target regions. To resolve this tension, we propose SAGE, a principled framework that enables controllable empirical support expansion by reshaping the reverse-KL anchor distribution itself through a guide function q(x,y), achieving consistent improvements in both pass@1 and pass@k across challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/tally0818/SAGE.

</details>


### [79] [From Sparsity to Simplicity: Enabling Simpler Sequential Replacements via Sparse Attention Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18865)
*Yuxin Ren,Maxwell D Collins,Miao Hu,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预训练Transformer中不同层的注意力稀疏度不同，稀疏层可被更简单的序列模块替代而不损失性能。通过基于注意力稀疏度的逐层蒸馏，实现了高效注意力替换，降低了参数量和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力的二次方复杂度导致推理昂贵，替换为简单序列模块在大规模下效果不佳。本文从稀疏性角度重新审视注意力替换，探索利用层间注意力稀疏度差异来实现无损替代。

Method: 提出即插即用的逐层蒸馏框架，基于预训练ViT的注意力稀疏度指导替换操作。先分析各层注意力稀疏模式，再对稀疏层用简单序列模块替换并通过蒸馏训练，同时采用AViT式token保留增强稀疏性。

Result: 稀疏层替换后准确率下降远小于稠密层；教师模型稀疏度越高，学生-教师差距越小。通过稀疏性引导的替换有效降低了参数量和推理延迟。

Conclusion: 注意力稀疏度可作为替换注意力模块的有效指导信号，在保持性能的前提下显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Self-attention serves as the core foundation of large-scale transformer pretraining, but its quadratic token interaction cost makes inference expensive. Replacing attention with simpler sequential modules is appealing, yet naive substitution is often lossy, especially at larger scales. This paper revisits attention replacement through the lens of sparsity. Based on the observation of diverse sparsity patterns across transformer layers, we posit that pretrained transformers decompose the complex token dependency across tokens into various sequence-to-sequence mappings of diverse complexities, where some layer functionalities can be approximated and replaced with much simpler sequential modules without loss. We evaluate this premise using a plug-and-play layer-wise distillation framework to approximate and replace attention functionalities in pretrained vision transformer models. Controlled group-wise replacements under a fixed training budget reveal a clear pattern: substituting layers with sparser attention incurs substantially smaller accuracy drops than replacing denser ones. We further impose explicit attention sparsity on the pretrained ViT via AViT-style token retention and perform sparsity-guided distillation for sequential replacing models, where we see increasing teacher sparsity consistently reduces the student-teacher gap. The proposed method achieves efficient attention replacement for reduced parameter size and latency through the guidance of attention sparsity.

</details>


### [80] [FLUIDSPLAT: Reconstructing Physical Fields from Sparse Sensors via Gaussian Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18866)
*Huaxi Huang,Meng Li,Zhengqing Gao,Xi Zhou,Xiaoshui Huang,Xiao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLUIDSPLAT是一种受3D高斯泼溅启发的传感器条件模型，通过预测K个各向异性高斯基元形成流场显式中间表示，并理论证明了在稀疏传感下基元数量存在方差瓶颈，从而引入状态条件残差解码器进行补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法将传感器读数编码为隐式潜码，缺乏空间可解释性，且缺乏表示容量随观测数量扩展的理论指导。

Method: 受3D高斯泼溅启发，提出FLUIDSPLAT模型，预测K个各向异性高斯基元形成单位划分支架(scaffold)，并结合状态条件残差解码器；理论上推导了高斯基元估计器的逼近率$O(K^{-s/d})$及含噪观测下的偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 在标准圆柱绕流基准上，FLUIDSPLAT在所有表面传感器布局下取得最佳平均误差；在AirfRANS数据集上使用8个表面压力传感器时，在三个标准划分上比最强基线降低11-23%误差。

Conclusion: 证明了稀疏传感下基元数量不能自由增长，存在方差瓶颈，提出显式中间表示结合残差解码器的有效框架。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous flow fields from sparse surface-mounted sensors is central to aerodynamic design, flow control, and digital-twin instrumentation. Existing neural methods for this task typically encode sensor readings into implicit latent codes with little spatial interpretability and limited formal guidance on how representational capacity should scale with observation count. Inspired by 3D Gaussian Splatting, we introduce FLUIDSPLAT, a sensor-conditioned model that predicts K anisotropic Gaussian primitives forming a partition-of-unity scaffold, a spatially explicit and interpretable intermediate representation of the flow. For an idealized Gaussian primitive estimator, we prove an $O(K^{-s/d})$ approximation rate for fields with Sobolev smoothness $s$; incorporating $N$ noisy observations yields a squared-risk decomposition with bias $O(K^{-2s/d})$ and variance $O(σ^{2}K/N)$.Balancing the two yields $K^{*}\!\sim\!(N/σ^{2})^{d/(2s+d)}$: primitive count cannot grow freely under sparse sensing, revealing a variance bottleneck that motivates complementing the scaffold with a state-conditioned residual decoder. On a standard cylinder-flow benchmark, FLUIDSPLAT achieves the best mean error across all surface-sensor layouts; on AirfRANS with 8 surface-pressure sensors, it reduces error by 11-23% over the strongest baseline across three standard splits.

</details>


### [81] [EVA-0: Test-Time Model Evolution with Only Two Forward Passes per Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18867)
*Guohao Chen,Shuaicheng Niu,Geng Li,Yunbei Zhang,Shilin Shan,Chunyan Miao,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EVA-0，一种无需反向传播的零阶测试时模型进化方法，仅需两次前向传播即可完成推理和适应，在ImageNet-C上超越现有方法并实现14倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时模型进化方法依赖反向传播，导致内存开销大，难以部署在边缘设备、量化模型、专用加速器或黑盒模型上。

Method: 提出EVA-0框架：(1)保持损失尺度不变性防止捷径解；(2)锚点引导优化策略缓解权重漂移；(3)逐样本对称双侧扰动估计更新方向。无需反向传播，仅需两次前向传播。

Result: 在ImageNet-C和ViT-Base上，EVA-0优于BP-based DeYO和BP-free FOA，相比FOA实现14倍加速。

Conclusion: 零阶优化在严格两次前向传播预算下可实现高效测试时模型适应，EVA-0为实际部署提供了一种轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time model evolution offers a promising way for deployed models to improve from unlabeled test-time experience, yet most existing methods depend on backpropagation (BP), which incurs substantial memory overhead and makes them difficult to deploy on edge devices, quantized models, specialized accelerators, or black-box models. In this work, we study test-time model evolution under a strict two-forward budget, a setting that pushes adaptation toward highly efficient real-world deployment. We reveal three key obstacles in zeroth-order test-time optimization: susceptibility to shortcut solutions, uncontrolled weight drift, and ineffective update direction estimation. To overcome them, we propose EVA-0, a minimal zeroth-order adaptation framework that: 1) keeps the loss scale-invariant to prevent shortcut solutions; 2) devises an anchor-guided optimization strategy to alleviate weight drift; 3) uses sample-wise symmetric two-sided perturbation for update direction estimation and inference. EVA-0 requires no BP and performs both inference and adaptation within only two forward passes per sample. Results on ImageNet-C & ViT-Base show that EVA-0 outperforms both BP-based DeYO and BP-free FOA, while achieving a 14x speed-up over FOA. Code will be released.

</details>


### [82] [MO-CAPO: Multi-Objective Cost-Aware Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18869)
*Jan Büssing,Moritz Schlager,Timo Heiß,Tom Zehle,Matthias Feurer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MO-CAPO，一种多目标提示优化算法，在性能与推理成本之间进行联合优化，利用预算分配提高优化效率，在四个任务和三个LLM上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化方法仅关注性能，忽略了推理成本或延迟等竞争性目标；而现有的多目标优化方法直接使用NSGA-II，优化效率低下。

Method: 提出MO-CAPO算法，实现性能与推理成本的联合优化，引入预算分配机制提升优化效率；设计了一个面向部署的成本目标函数，全面捕捉LLM推理的计算特征。

Result: 在12个案例中，MO-CAPO在8个上优于NSGA-II基线（基于噪声R2指标）；能在较低预算下取得有竞争力的性能；发现的解集覆盖了多样化的性能-成本权衡。

Conclusion: MO-CAPO能够高效地为从业者提供一组具有不同性能-成本权衡的提示方案，便于实际部署选择。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks but are highly sensitive to prompt design, motivating the need for automatic prompt optimization. Existing methods predominantly focus on performance alone, ignoring competing objectives such as inference cost or latency. At the same time, existing work on multi-objective prompt optimization relies on off-the-shelf NSGA-II, ignoring optimization efficiency. As a remedy, we introduce MO-CAPO, a novel multi-objective prompt optimization algorithm that jointly optimizes performance and inference cost while leveraging budget allocation for cost-efficient optimization. We further propose a deployment-oriented cost objective that captures the full computational profile of LLM inference. We evaluate our approach across four tasks and three LLMs and compare it to an NSGA-II-based multi-objective method and state-of-the-art single-objective prompt optimizers. Results show that MO-CAPO consistently identifies strong, robust, and diverse Pareto front approximations while maintaining cost-efficiency. It outperforms the NSGA-II baseline on 8 out of 12 cases in terms of the noisy R2 metric and achieves competitive performances often already at a considerably lower budget. The discovered solution sets span diverse performance-cost trade-offs that are omitted by single-objective optimizers, yet the top-performance candidates remain competitive with single-objective solutions. Additionally, we conduct the first evaluation of multi-objective machine learning experiments that considers generalization and robustness through noisy R2 and approximation gap, enabling a more realistic assessment of solution quality. MO-CAPO enables practitioners to select from an efficiently discovered set of multiple prompts offering different trade-offs between performance and cost.

</details>


### [83] [Multi-Headed Transformer Architectures as Time-dependent Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18870)
*Alex Massucco,Leonardo Del Grande,Marcello Carioni,Christoff Brune,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过时间依赖梯度流模型刻画多头Transformer架构中的数据流，将注意力机制建模为交互能量，证明了在权重可积条件下梯度流的ω-极限集是能量驻点，并分析了初值和权重扰动下的稳定性，最后通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论模型与实际Transformer架构之间存在较大差距，且依赖于强简化假设，缺乏对多头注意力机制和多层、多权重情形的直接数学刻画。

Method: 将多头Transformer中的数据流建模为时间依赖的梯度流，交互能量反映注意力机制设计；允许每层每头不同权重且无初始化约束；证明ω-极限集为驻点；分析初值和权重扰动下的稳定性（连续依赖性与Γ-收敛）。

Result: 理论上证明了梯度流的ω-极限集是交互能量在极限权重分布下的驻点；建立了对噪声输入的连续依赖性和流的唯一性；证明了扰动能量的Γ-收敛性。数值实验验证了能量耗散恒等式及自治（Ornstein-Uhlenbeck）与非自治（振荡权重）两种动力学行为。

Conclusion: 该理论框架有效缩小了实际Transformer架构与数学模型之间的鸿沟，为理解多头注意力机制的动力学行为提供了严格的理论基础，并具有良好的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In recent years, transformer architectures have revolutionized the field of language processing, opening the door to previously unforeseen possibilities. However, from a theoretical point of view, the mathematical models proposed in the literature often lack direct contact with the actual architectures and depend on strong simplifying assumptions. In this paper, we reduce this gap by modelling the data flow in multi-headed transformer architectures as time-dependent gradient flows for a suitable interaction energy capturing the design of the attention mechanism. The explicit dependence on time allows us to consider different weights for each head and for each layer, without imposing constraints on the initialization method. Moreover, we prove that, under a suitable integrability assumption on the evolution of the weights, each element of the $ω$-limit set of the gradient flows is a stationary point of the interaction energy at a limiting weight distribution. Finally, we analyse the stability of the gradient flows considering perturbations of both the initial data and the weights. Specifically, on the one hand, we study the robustness of the proposed models with respect to noisy inputs, establishing a continuous dependence of the gradient flows on the initial data and uniqueness of the flows. On the other hand, we prove the $Γ$-convergence of the perturbed interaction energy to the unperturbed one, leading to the convergence of the corresponding gradient flows. We complement these theoretical results with numerical experiments that confirm the predicted energy-dissipation identity and clarify the asymptotic behavior of the dynamics in both the autonomous-like (Ornstein--Uhlenbeck) and the genuinely non-autonomous (oscillating-weights) regimes.

</details>


### [84] [ZeroUnlearn: Few-Shot Knowledge Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18879)
*Yujie Lin,Chengyi Yang,Zhishang Xiang,Yiping Song,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ZeroUnlearn框架，通过模型编辑将机器遗忘重构为精确的知识重映射问题，采用乘法参数更新和封闭解实现高效、有针对性的遗忘，同时保持模型整体效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在训练过程中不可避免会保留敏感信息，可能导致有害生成，而现有机器遗忘方法存在计算成本高或容易破坏相关知识和模型效用的问题。

Method: 将机器遗忘重构为知识重映射问题，提出ZeroUnlearn框架，通过乘法参数更新和封闭解实现表征正交性，并扩展出基于梯度的变体以处理多样本遗忘。

Result: 该方法在多个基准上优于现有方法，同时保持模型通用效用。

Conclusion: ZeroUnlearn通过模型编辑实现了高效、精确的机器遗忘，在不牺牲模型整体性能的前提下有效移除敏感信息。

Abstract: Large language models inevitably retain sensitive information, defined as inputs that may induce harmful generations, due to training on massive web corpora, raising concerns for privacy and safety. Existing machine unlearning methods primarily rely on retraining or aggressive fine-tuning, which are either computationally expensive or prone to degrading related knowledge and overall model utility. In this work, we reformulate machine unlearning as a precise knowledge re-mapping problem via model editing. We propose ZeroUnlearn, a few-shot unlearning framework. It overwrites sensitive inputs by mapping them to a neutral target state and removing their original representations. ZeroUnlearn enforces representational orthogonality through a multiplicative parameter update with a closed-form solution, enabling efficient and targeted unlearning. We further extend ZeroUnlearn to a gradient-based variant for multi-sample unlearning. Experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing baselines while preserving general model utility. Our code is available at the github: https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/ZeroUnlearn.

</details>


### [85] [A Multi-Dimensional Clustering Approach for Identifying Inborn Errors of Immunity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18880)
*Nishad Kulkarni,Alexandra K. Martinson,Nicholas L. Rider,Michael Keller,Syed Muhammad Anwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套结合数据整理和机器学习聚类算法的管道，用于从国家数据注册库中识别罕见疾病（如先天性免疫缺陷）的模式和特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病（如先天性免疫缺陷）需要早期诊断以防止器官损伤并改善生活质量，但大规模电子健康记录数据的获取和整理存在困难，且机器学习在免疫缺陷领域的模式识别和复杂数据处理方法有限。

Method: 构建了一个包含数据整理和机器学习聚类算法的管道，将原始免疫学实验室数据转化为向量，并结合超参数调优进行疾病模式识别的聚类分析。

Result: 该管道能够从国家数据注册库中识别新的罕见病模式并提取免疫缺陷相关特征，提升了特征认知，开发了罕见病群体分析的数据工具包。

Conclusion: 本研究为转化复杂医疗记录为无监督机器学习可解释的数据结构提供了方法，拓展了罕见病数据驱动分析的前沿。

Abstract: Rare diseases such as inborn errors of immunity (IEI) require early diagnosis to prevent end organ damage and improve quality of life. Hurdles in accessing and curating large scale electronic health record (EHR) data limit routine data driven analyses to remain on the forefront of IEI and other rare disease trends. Development of machine learning (ML) algorithms in IEI for pattern recognition as well as published methodology examining how to systematically process and integrate complex medical data is limited. Our proposed pipeline, including data curation and ML clustering algorithms, is designed to recognize novel rare disease patterns and extract IEI- associated features from a national data registry. Our methodology for EHR data formatting and processing presents the pipeline that transforms raw immunologic lab data into vectors. This is further combined with hyperparameter tuning for diseases pattern recognition via clustering. This study refines IEI feature awareness, develops data tool kits for rare disease populations analysis, and expands on transforming complex medical records in data structures interpretable by unsupervised ML.

</details>


### [86] [Emergence of a Flow-Assisted Casting Strategy for Olfactory Navigation via Memory-Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18881)
*Changxu Zhao,Dongxiao Zhao,Xin Bian,Gaojin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过强化学习智能体在非稳态流场中的导航实验，发现存在最优记忆窗口长度，智能体发展出流辅助的"扇形搜索"策略，并自适应调整搜索轨迹和浓度阈值，平均趋近速度随记忆长度呈非单调变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究动物在动态流场中利用随机探测信号进行气味搜索时，存在最优积分时间窗口的底层机制。

Method: 使用强化学习（RL）智能体在非稳态流场中，通过改变记忆长度和流场条件，研究其导航性能，无需预定义模型。

Result: 智能体发展了流辅助的"扇形搜索"（sector-search）策略，自适应调整搜索轨迹几何形状和触发搜索的浓度阈值；平均趋近速度随记忆长度呈非单调变化。

Conclusion: 发现最优记忆窗口的存在，并提出了"扇形搜索"模型解释非单调依赖关系，揭示了动态流场中气味搜索效率与记忆长度的内在联系。

Abstract: In dynamic flow fields, various animals exhibit remarkable odor search capabilities despite relying on stochastic detections. Interestingly, there exists an optimal time window for integrating these detections that maximizes search efficiency. To understand the underlying mechanism, we investigate the navigation performance of Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents in unsteady flows under varying memory lengths and flow conditions. Without any predefined models, the agents develop a flow-assisted casting strategy and adaptively adjust both the geometry of their search trajectories and the concentration threshold for initiating casting to maximize the success rate. The agent's average speed toward the odor source exhibits a non-monotonic dependence on memory length, which can be explained by the "sector-search" model.

</details>


### [87] [To Call or Not to Call: Diagnosing Intrinsic Over-Calling Bias in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18882)
*Wei Shi,Ziheng Peng,Sihang Li,Xiting Wang,Xiang Wang,Mengnan Du,Na Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM智能体存在过度调用工具的倾向，通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）诊断出内在偏差（IBH），并提出自适应边际校准引导（AMCS）方法进行因果修正，有效缓解过度调用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在调用工具时表现出系统性过度调用倾向，即在不需要工具的场景下仍倾向于调用，导致整体准确率仅55%-70%。现有研究多将过度调用视为经验现象，缺乏对其机制层面的理解和因果修正方法。

Method: 提出内在偏差假说（IBH），认为调用/不调用决策中存在与激活无关的偏移量。使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）恢复行为对齐的特征基，将其简化为带符号激活边际，直接估计偏移量。进一步提出自适应边际校准引导（AMCS）方法，沿SAE解码器方向施加闭环反偏置平移，抵消诊断出的偏移。

Result: 在六个模型上验证了IBH：模型仅在"不调用"激活显著超过"调用"激活时才处于决策中性状态。AMCS方法有效缓解过度调用，在调用准确率几乎不下降的情况下提升整体准确率。

Conclusion: 将过度调用从经验现象重新定义为可因果修正的机制对象，提供了SAE驱动的诊断与闭环校正框架。

Abstract: LLM agents exhibit a consistent tendency to over-call, invoking tools even in situations where none is needed. On the When2Call benchmark, six models from three families show high call accuracy but much lower no-call accuracy, leaving overall accuracy in the 55%-70% range. We trace this to an Intrinsic Bias Hypothesis (IBH): the call/no-call decision mapping carries an activation-independent call offset, so the model favors call even at activation parity. Using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), we recover behavior-aligned feature bases for the call/no_call decision, reduce them to a signed activation margin, and estimate the offset directly. Across all six models, the model is decision-neutral only when no_call activation outweighs call activation, consistent with IBH. We then causally test IBH with Adaptive Margin-Calibrated Steering (AMCS), a closed-form counter-bias shift along SAE decoder directions. Cancelling the diagnosed offset mitigates over-calling and improves overall accuracy with a negligible drop in call accuracy. Our work recasts over-calling from an empirical phenomenon into a mechanistic object amenable to causal correction. Code is available at https://github.com/SKURA502/agent-sae/.

</details>


### [88] [Robust Checkpoint Selection for Multimodal LLMs via Agentic Evaluation and Stability-Aware Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18852)
*Qinwu Xu,Zhuoheng Li,Jessie Salas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多阶段框架，通过结合真实世界数据、结构化LLM评判和渐进式排序协议（点过滤、列排序、对比较），并引入基于子采样的置信度估计和百分位评分公式，解决了多模态大模型在评估不确定性下的检查点选择问题，尤其适用于OCR密集场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖静态基准或点式评分，在性能差异微小且评估信号易受噪声干扰时，难以与真实使用场景对齐，且缺乏鲁棒的不确定性估计，尤其在OCR密集型场景下问题突出。

Method: 将检查点选择形式化为评估不确定性下的鲁棒决策问题，提出多阶段框架：①基于筛选的真实世界数据；②结构化LLM判断；③多阶段排序协议（点过滤→列排序→对比较）；④引入子采样置信度估计与百分位评分，捕获分布特征并惩罚尾部失效。

Result: 验证了数据质量（尤其OCR可读性）是评估有效性的关键决定因素，提出的框架在OCR密集场景下优于现有静态基准和点式评分方法。

Conclusion: 多阶段渐进式排序结合置信度估计能有效提升MLLM检查点选择的鲁棒性，且评估数据的OCR质量对结果可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: Checkpoint selection for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) presents significant challenges when performance differentials are marginal and evaluation signals are prone to noise. Existing methodologies rely heavily on static benchmarks or pointwise scoring, which frequently misalign with in-the-wild usage and lack robust uncertainty estimation, particularly in OCR-heavy scenarios. In this work, we formulate checkpoint selection as a robust decision problem under evaluation uncertainty. We propose a multi-stage framework that integrates curated real-world data, structured LLM-based judgment, and multi-stage ranking protocols. The evaluation system orchestrates progressive refinement via pointwise filtering, listwise ranking, and pairwise comparison. To enhance reliability, we introduce subsampling-based confidence estimation and a percentile-based scoring formulation that captures distributional characteristics while penalizing tail failures. Furthermore, we demonstrate that data quality, specifically OCR readability, is a critical determinant of evaluation validity.

</details>


### [89] [Navigating the Emotion Tree: Hierarchical Hyperbolic RAG for Multimodal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18884)
*Zeheng Wang,Bo Zhao,Yijie Zhu,Zhishu Liu,Hui Ma,Ruixin Zhang,Shouhong Ding,Qianyu Xie,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperEmo-RAG框架，通过分层双曲嵌入和结构化证据注入，解决多模态情感识别中忽略情感层次结构及缺乏外部上下文知识的问题，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型将情感类别视为独立标签，忽略了情感分类学中的层次树状结构；同时缺乏外部上下文知识，容易过度解读噪声线索，导致细粒度情感分类困难。

Method: 1) 分层双曲接地：将情感标签和多模态样本嵌入连续双曲空间（庞加莱球），设计分层波束搜索逐步从粗到细检索样本。2) 结构化证据注入：构建证据图，通过Tree-Aware Attention和EmotionGraphFormer将结构化知识注入LLM。

Result: 在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: HyperEmo-RAG通过利用结构化情感知识库和双曲空间的分层特性，有效提升了多模态情感识别的细粒度分类性能。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition aims to integrate text, audio, and video sources to understand human affective states. Although multimodal large language models excel at multimodal reasoning, they typically treat emotion categories as independent labels, ignoring the rich hierarchical taxonomy of human psychology. Moreover, lacking external contextual knowledge makes them highly susceptible to over-interpreting noisy cues, further complicating fine-grained emotion classification. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{HyperEmo-RAG}, a retrieval-augmented generation framework that leverages a structured emotional knowledge base. Our framework introduces two key innovations. 1) Hierarchical hyperbolic grounding. Recognizing the inherent hierarchical tree structure of emotion taxonomies, we jointly embed hierarchical emotion labels and multimodal samples into a continuous hyperbolic space (Poincaré ball) and design a hierarchical beam-search deliberation process that progressively retrieves samples from coarse to fine-grained levels. 2) Structured evidence injection. Based on the retrieved evidence, we construct an evidence graph and inject the structured knowledge as explicit cognitive context into the LLM through a Tree-Aware Attention mechanism and an EmotionGraphFormer, preserving the integrity of graph-structured information. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that HyperEmo-RAG significantly outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [90] [Auditing Reasoning-Trace Memorization Claims after Unlearning with Head-Conditioned Canaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18891)
*Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了推理模型中"绕过模式"（bypass pattern）作为权重记忆证据的有效性，发现仅靠解析器分割的绕过差距无法可靠判断模型是否真正遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法将推理模型中思考痕迹仍输出被遗忘内容而回答侧已遗忘的现象解释为权重仍记住，但缺乏对这种解释可靠性的严格审计。

Method: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上使用LoRA记忆虚构作者，用NPO进行遗忘，结合六令牌哨兵头条件，通过交换思考痕迹为短非哨兵前缀来测试绕过差距。

Result: 发现绕过差距在某些种子下消失，在另一些种子下甚至方向反转；不同蒸馏模型上指标因解析器找不到闭合标签而符号翻转。

Conclusion: 正解析器分割绕过差距既不能证明也不能排除权重级记忆，建议在规范审计之外增加解码时模板交换作为廉价健全性检查。

Abstract: Evaluations of unlearning on reasoning models sometimes show a bypass pattern. The answer side looks unlearned, but the model's own thinking trace keeps emitting the forgotten content, and the gap is taken as evidence that the weights still remember. We audit this reading on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B with LoRA-memorized fictional authors and NPO unlearning, conditioned on a six-token canary head. On one seed, swapping the thinking trace for a short non-canary prefill on the same weights drops the answer rate by as much as the bypass gap itself, whether the prefill mimics the training template or not. On a second seed the bypass gap shrinks rather than vanishing, and the prefill swap reverses direction and brings the answer rate to ceiling. A positive parser-split bypass gap thus does not by itself identify hidden weight-level memorization, and does not rule it out either. On a different distillate the same metric flips sign because the parser cannot find the closing tag. We recommend a decode-time template swap as a cheap sanity check alongside the canonical audit.

</details>


### [91] [Data-Free Client Contribution Estimation via Logit Maximization for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18892)
*Asim Ukaye,Nurbek Tastan,Mubarak Abdu-Aguye,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于logit最大化的数据无关、按类别贡献估计与聚合框架(CELM)，用于解决联邦学习中类别不平衡和标签偏移问题，在不额外交换数据的情况下提升少数类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习实际部署中常出现严重的类别不平衡和标签偏移，标准聚合协议会过拟合主导客户端，降低少数类性能，而现有方法需要共享数据或元数据。

Method: 服务器通过探测客户端更新获得类别证据分数，构建跨客户端证据矩阵，量化每类能力和类别覆盖度，在此基础上计算贡献权重，对提供强判别证据的少数类客户端进行上加权，并使用单纯形约束和动量平滑保证稳定性。

Result: 在代表性视觉基准上，在非IID和病理性标签划分条件下，CELM聚合方法相比基线提升了在不平衡和统计异质性下的鲁棒性，取得更好性能且无需额外数据交换。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题，兼容标准训练流程，具有良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative learning of computer vision models, where privacy and regulatory constraints prevent centralizing data across devices or organizations. However, practical FL deployments often exhibit severe class imbalance and label skew, causing standard aggregation protocols to overfit dominant clients and degrade minority-class performance. We propose a data-free, class-wise contribution estimation and aggregation framework based on logit maximization (CELM) that does not require sharing raw data, client metadata, or auxiliary public datasets. The FL server probes client updates to obtain class-wise evidence scores and assembles a cross-client evidence matrix, which quantifies both per-class competence and class coverage. Using this matrix, we compute contribution weights that upweight clients providing strong, discriminative evidence for underrepresented classes. The resulting aggregation is stable due to simplex constraints and momentum smoothing, and it remains compatible with standard FL training pipelines. We evaluate the approach on representative vision benchmarks under controlled non-IID and pathological label splits, demonstrating that CELM-based aggregation improves robustness to imbalance and statistical heterogeneity, while yielding better performance without requiring any additional data exchange.

</details>


### [92] [Dynamic Model Merging Made Slim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18904)
*Guodong Du,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiDi-Merging框架，通过可微秩分配在低秩模块中平衡共享参数与专家参数，实现模型合并的高效性，在仅1.24倍单模型参数下达到基线性能，1.4倍时超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态模型合并方法存在精度-效率权衡不佳的问题：要么维护一个完整共享模型加上小专家，要么为专家分配过多容量，导致次优效果。

Method: 提出可微秩分配机制，将参数预算优化转化为低秩模块中的可微秩优化问题；并引入数据无关的精炼步骤以恢复任务保真度。

Result: 仅需1.24倍单微调模型参数即达到基线水平，1.4倍时超越基线，比需要2倍以上存储的方法更紧凑。

Conclusion: DiDi-Merging在视觉、语言和多模态任务上均有效，实现了更优的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Model merging enables the reuse of fine-tuned models without joint training or access to original data. Dynamic merging further improves flexibility by selectively activating task-relevant parameters and efficiently composing experts across multiple tasks. However, existing dynamic methods either maintain a full shared model with tiny experts or allocate excessive capacity to experts, leading to suboptimal accuracy--efficiency trade-offs. To address this, we propose DiDi-Merging, a slim dynamic merging framework that leverages differentiable rank allocation to balance shared and expert parameters. By formulating parameter budgeting as differentiable rank optimization in low-rank modules and introducing a data-free refinement step to recover task fidelity, DiDi-Merging matches prior dynamic baselines at only 1.24x the parameters of a single fine-tuned model and surpasses them at 1.4x, substantially more compact than methods requiring > 2x storage. DiDi-Merging applies across vision, language, and multimodal tasks.

</details>


### [93] [Position: Graph Condensation Needs a Reset -- Move Beyond Full-dataset Training and Model-Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18893)
*Mridul Gupta,Samyak Jain,Vansh Ramani,Hariprasad Kodamana,Sayan Ranu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前图压缩（Graph Condensation）方法存在根本性矛盾：需要全数据集训练才能生成压缩图，且存在高计算开销、跨架构泛化差、评估指标误导等问题。作者呼吁该领域需要重新定位，转向轻量级、架构无关、可实际部署的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图压缩方法虽然旨在解决GNN在大规模图上的可扩展性问题，但主流梯度匹配方法存在根本性矛盾——需要在整个原始数据集上训练才能生成压缩图，这违背了效率优化的初衷；同时存在高计算开销、跨架构泛化差、评估指标误导等系统性问题。

Method: 本文为立场论文（position paper），不是提出新方法，而是系统性地批判当前图压缩领域的方法论缺陷，包括：梯度匹配的根本矛盾、计算开销问题、跨架构泛化不足、节点压缩比等评估指标的误导性，以及神经架构搜索中的虚假应用。在此基础上提出了重新定位的研究方向。

Result: 揭示了当前图压缩领域的系统性缺陷：主流方法需要全数据集训练导致效率目标自相矛盾；计算开销高；跨GNN架构泛化能力差；评估指标（如节点压缩比）具有误导性；在NAS等应用场景中存在虚假承诺。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁图压缩领域进行根本性重置，即放弃依赖全数据集训练和模型依赖的设计范式，转向轻量级、架构无关、可实际部署的方法，以实现真正的可扩展GNN训练。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are powerful tools for learning from graph-structured data, but their scalability is increasingly strained by the size of real-world graphs in domains like recommender systems, fraud detection, and molecular biology. Graph condensation -- the task of generating a smaller synthetic graph that retains the performance of models trained on the original -- has emerged as a promising solution. However, the dominant approach of gradient matching introduces a fundamental contradiction: it requires training on the full dataset to create the compressed version, thereby undermining the goal of efficiency. Worse still, these methods suffer from high computational overhead, poor generalization across GNN architectures, and brittle reliance on specific model configurations. Equally concerning is the community's reliance on misleading evaluation protocols such as node compression ratios, which fail to reflect true resource savings, condensation overhead, and illusory application to neural architecture search. These shortcomings are not incidental -- they are systemic, and they obstruct meaningful progress.
  In this position paper, we argue that graph condensation, in its current form, needs a reset. We call for moving beyond full-dataset training and model-dependent design, and instead advocate for methods that are lightweight, architecture-agnostic, and practically deployable. By identifying key methodological flaws and outlining concrete research directions, we aim to reorient the field toward approaches that deliver on the true promise of condensation: efficient, generalizable, and usable GNN training at scale.

</details>


### [94] [FedMental: Evaluating Federated Learning for Mental Health Detection from Social Media Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18936)
*Nuredin Ali Abdelkadir,Anjali Ratnam,Zeerak Talat,Stevie Chancellor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了联邦学习和差分隐私联邦学习在心理健康预测任务（抑郁症检测和自杀危机检测）中的隐私保护效果与性能权衡，发现联邦学习性能接近集中式训练，但差分隐私联邦学习即使低噪声水平下也会导致显著的性能下降（F1最多下降27.01），原因是其扭曲了心理健康相关的稀疏语言标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体文本数据广泛用于训练识别高风险心理健康行为的机器学习模型，但分享这类敏感数据存在隐私风险，限制了基准数据集的增长。作者希望评估隐私保护机器学习技术能否在保障性能的同时实现更安全的数据共享。

Method: 在Twitter上进行抑郁症检测、在Reddit上进行自杀危机检测；将每个用户视为非独立同分布（non-IID）客户端；评估不同的客户端比例、聚合策略和隐私预算；对比集中式训练、联邦学习和差分隐私联邦学习的性能。

Result: 联邦学习（F1=83.16）性能接近集中式训练（F1=85.63）；但差分隐私联邦学习即使噪声水平较低（epsilon=50）也会导致高达27.01的F1下降；性能下降的原因是差分隐私扭曲了与心理健康高度相关但稀疏的语言标记（如健康话题词和情绪词）。

Conclusion: 当前隐私保护技术（尤其是差分隐私联邦学习）在心理健康推理任务中存在性能与隐私之间的巨大权衡，有其潜力但也存在明显局限性。

Abstract: Social media text data are often used to train Machine Learning (ML) models to identify users exhibiting high-risk mental health behaviors. However, sharing this sensitive data poses privacy risks and limits the growth of benchmark datasets. We comprehensively evaluate whether privacy-preserving ML techniques can enable safer data sharing while preserving performance. Specifically, we apply federated learning (FL) and Differentially Private FL for two widely-studied mental health prediction tasks: depression detection on X (Twitter) and suicide crisis detection on Reddit. We simulate realistic data-sharing scenarios by treating each user as a client in a non-IID setting, evaluating across different client fractions, aggregation strategies, and privacy budgets. While FL achieves comparable performance to centralized training (centralized F1 = 85.63; best FL model F1 = 83.16) on depression identification, we find that Differentially Private FL has a large performance-privacy trade-off (up to F1 = 27.01 drop) even with low levels of noise (epsilon = 50). This is due to the distortion of highly informative yet sparse mental health linguistic markers related to mental health, like health topics and emotion words. This research empirically demonstrates the potential and limitations of current privacy preservation techniques for mental health inference tasks.

</details>


### [95] [A Two-Parameter Weibull Framework for Diagnosing Transformer Weight Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18898)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用威布尔分布（极值理论中的两参数族）作为诊断框架，分析Transformer中逐元素的权重幅度分布。通过拟合12个模型（7种架构家族）的权重矩阵，发现FFN和注意力输出投影（传输类）的k值集中在窄带[1.186,1.204]；注意力输入投影（选择类）偏离威布尔族，偏离程度受存储方式影响；λ在训练中增长，与sqrt(η/λ_wd)强相关。k标识功能类，λ标识训练进度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚合统计量无法解析Transformer中各组件、各层、各训练步的权重分布动态，需要一个架构无关的诊断工具来揭示训练过程中的细粒度变化。

Method: 用威布尔分布拟合每层每个权重矩阵的幅度分布，通过中间80%概率图拟合得到形状参数k和尺度参数λ，对Pythia、OLMo-1/2、LLaMA-3、Mistral、Qwen2.5/3共12个模型进行检查点的逐层分析。

Result: 1) FFN和W_o（传输类）的k值高度稳定（中位数1.186-1.204，跨家族CV=0.51%）；2) W_q/W_k（选择类）偏离威布尔族，偏离程度由KV存储方式决定；3) λ随训练增长，与sqrt(η/λ_wd)强相关（Pearson r=0.94）。

Conclusion: 威布尔分布参数k和λ携带独立信息，分别标识权重矩阵的功能类别和训练进展，为Transformer训练动力学提供了统一、架构无关的诊断工具。

Abstract: We apply the Weibull distribution -- a two-parameter family from extreme-value theory -- as a diagnostic framework for element-wise weight magnitude distributions in transformers. At initialization, i.i.d. Gaussian weights give |w| ~ HalfNormal, yielding k ~ 1.20 via middle-80% probability-plot fit (the protocol used throughout this work). This anchor makes k a principled, architecture-independent measuring stick for training dynamics; fitting each weight matrix independently at every layer at every checkpoint enables per-component, per-layer, and per-step diagnostics that aggregate statistics cannot resolve.
  Applying this framework to 12 model entries spanning 7 architectural families (Pythia, OLMo-1/2, LLaMA-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5/3) reveals three findings. First, FFN modules and the attention output projection W_o -- the Transmission Class -- fall in a narrow k band: median terminal k in [1.186, 1.204] across 12 entries (cross-family CV = 0.51%), shared across SwiGLU/GeLU activations, Pre-LN/QK-Norm placements, and 70M-14B sizes. Second, the attention input projections W_q, W_k -- the Selection Class -- depart from the Weibull family, with severity shaped by storage: separately-stored Q/K (OLMo-1, OLMo-2) yields k in [0.76, 0.99] (deep); GQA models yield k in [1.10, 1.16] (mild); Pythia's merged W_qkv occupies a transitional zone tracking training budget T/tau monotonically. Third, lambda grows substantially during training and scales with sqrt(eta/lambda_wd) within the Pythia family (Pearson r = 0.94, three Transmission kinds), directionally consistent with Fan et al. (2025). The two parameters carry independent information: k labels the functional class, lambda labels training progress.
  We release npm-weibull-py v0.4 (Python library) and DATABASE_v9_1 at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public .

</details>


### [96] [Don't Let Bandit Feedback Pull Continual LLM-Recommender Updates Off Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18899)
*Taesan Kim,Hyeongjun Yun,Jaegul Choo,Chung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ABPO框架，通过锚定波段优化和不对称反馈处理，解决LLM推荐系统持续更新中的曝光偏差和反馈模糊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式LLM推荐系统需要持续更新，但部署日志仅提供策略条件化的上下文赌博反馈，存在曝光偏差和反馈信号不对称（正反馈可靠，无响应模糊）的问题。

Method: 将已曝光推荐作为锚定项插入GRPO组，进行组相对归一化校准；对锚定项应用自归一化逆倾向评分校正策略偏差；对无响应使用模型输出置信度作为自确定性信号削弱惩罚强度。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews和MovieLens五个领域上，方法持续提升推荐准确率，且比基线更有效缓解曝光偏差。

Conclusion: ABPO框架能有效处理持续更新中的曝光偏差和反馈模糊问题，提升LLM推荐系统的更新效果。

Abstract: Generative LLM-based recommenders (LLM-Rec) require continual post-deployment updates, yet deployment logs provide only policy-shaped contextual bandit feedback: outcomes are observed solely for items exposed by a prior serving policy, inducing exposure bias and yielding partial, asymmetric signals consisting of relatively reliable positive responses and ambiguous no-responses. We propose an Anchored Bandit Policy Optimization (ABPO) framework for continual LLM-Rec updates that combines group-relative policy optimization (GRPO) with explicit treatment of exposure bias and feedback ambiguity. Specifically, we insert the exposed recommendation as a logged anchor into each GRPO rollout group, so that group-relative normalization is calibrated against the action actually exposed by the prior policy rather than against newly sampled rollouts alone. Because both positive- and no-responses are observed only through prior-policy exposure, we apply self-normalized inverse propensity scoring to the fixed anchor for both feedback types to correct for policy mismatch. At the same time, we treat the two feedback types asymmetrically in reliability: positive responses provide relatively direct endorsement signals, whereas no-responses remain ambiguous because they may reflect either true disinterest or unobserved external factors. To avoid overly aggressive updates from ambiguous no-responses, we temper their penalties with self-certainty, using the model's output-token confidence as a verifier-free reliability signal. Across five domains from Amazon Reviews and MovieLens, our method yields consistent post-update gains in recommendation accuracy while mitigating prior-policy-induced exposure bias more effectively than prior baselines.

</details>


### [97] [Counterfactual Likelihood Tests for Indirect Influence in Private Reasoning Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19092)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种反事实似然检验方法，用于测量私有推理通道之间的间接影响，并通过实验验证了公共通道是影响传递的完整载体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理系统将中间计算分为私有和公共通道，但评估时难以区分独立共推导、直接访问私有内容和通过公共通信的间接影响，缺乏可靠的测量方法。

Method: 采用反事实似然检验：用长度匹配的捐赠块替换上游私有块，固定公共词序列和下游目标，测量下游目标负对数似然的变化。通过长度匹配控制RoPE位置混淆，使用掩码和未掩码条件进行对比。

Result: 文本探针不可靠（n-gram重叠高估、校正重叠有噪声、金丝雀复现无区分度）；反向B-to-A影响近乎为零，而A-to-B影响通过公共语音隐藏状态持续存在；图分离控制验证了公共通道是反事实信号的完整载体。

Conclusion: 私有通道评估应分别报告直接和间接影响，反事实似然探针可作为测量这些边界的实用默认方法。

Abstract: Reasoning systems increasingly separate intermediate computation into private and public channels, creating evaluation cases that look similar in transcripts: independent co-derivation, direct access to private content, and indirect influence through public communication. This paper presents a counterfactual likelihood test for measuring influence between private reasoning channels. The method replaces an upstream private block with a length-matched donor block, holds the public token sequence and downstream target fixed, and measures the downstream target's negative-log-likelihood shift. On a 7B role-channel reasoning model used for validation, textual probes are unreliable: raw n-gram overlap overstates leakage, corrected overlap remains noisy, and canary reproduction reports no discrimination. Counterfactual likelihood separates unmasked and masked conditions, while length matching controls a RoPE positional confound. In the hardened masked validation, reverse B-to-A influence is near zero, while A-to-B influence persists through public-speech hidden states. A multi-checkpoint validation across three checkpoints, five seeds, and 13,734 valid directional contrasts replicates this asymmetry. A graph-separation control that blocks private-to-public carrier edges produces bit-identical natural and counterfactual scores across all 13,734 control evaluations, identifying the tested public-channel pathway as the complete carrier of the measured counterfactual signal under the implemented role-visibility mask. The results show that private-channel evaluation should report direct and indirect influence separately, and that counterfactual likelihood probes provide a practical default for measuring these boundaries.

</details>


### [98] [Reasoning Portability: Guiding Continual Learning for MLLMs in the RLVR Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18903)
*Qiuhe Hong,Yuyang Liu,Shuo Yang,Tiantian Peng,Fei Zhu,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于推理可移植性的动态平衡持续学习方法（RDB-CL），通过样本级推理约束来优化多模态大语言模型在持续学习中的表现，相比基线方法在Last准确率上提升12.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型与可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）结合的持续学习范式缺乏有效的推理级约束机制，传统答案级信号在分布外样本上不可靠，需要一种新的方法来引导持续适应。

Method: 定义推理可移植性（RP）作为样本级度量，衡量旧策略行为在新任务上的可复用程度；提出RDB-CL方法，根据RP值动态调节每个样本上的KL正则化强度：高RP样本使用紧锚点保留可复用推理，低RP样本使用松锚点允许探索新推理路径。

Result: RDB-CL在所有实验设置中一致优于基线方法，相较于原始RLVR基线，Last准确率提升12.0%。

Conclusion: 推理级信号在分布外样本上比答案级信号更可靠，基于推理可移植性的动态平衡策略可以有效提升多模态大语言模型持续学习性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models in Continual Learning (VLM-CL) aim to continuously adapt to new multimodal tasks while retaining prior knowledge. The emerging paradigm that couples Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) with Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) calls for a new pattern to guide continual adaptation. Advances in reasoning capability now make it feasible to impose constraints at the reasoning level. We formalize portability, a sample-level measure of how reusable the previous policy's behavior is on a new task, and empirically show that reasoning-level signals remain reliable on out-of-distribution samples while answer-level signals do not. We instantiate this as Reasoning Portability (RP) and propose Reasoning-based Dynamic Balance Continual Learning (RDB-CL), which modulates the per-sample Kullback-Leibler regularization in RLVR according to RP: a tight anchor preserves reusable reasoning on high-RP samples, while a relaxed anchor on low-RP samples permits exploration of new reasoning pathways. Experiments show that RDB-CL consistently outperforms baselines, improving Last accuracy by +12.0% over the vanilla RLVR baseline.

</details>


### [99] [EgoBabyVLM: Benchmarking Cross-Modal Learning from Naturalistic Egocentric Video Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19130)
*Dongyan Lin,Phillip Rust,Angel Villar Corrales,Alvin W. M. Tan,Mahi Luthra,Charles-Éric Saint-James,Rashel Moritz,Sheila Krogh-Jespersen,Vanessa Stark,Surya Parimi,Jiayi Shen,Youssef Benchekroun,Yosuke Higuchi,Martin Gleize,Tom Fizycki,Nicolas Hamilakis,Manel Khentout,Sho Tsuji,Balázs Kégl,Juan Pino,Michael C. Frank,Emmanuel Dupoux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当前最先进的视觉语言模型（VLM）在从稀疏、弱对齐的自我中心视频流（如婴儿头戴摄像头的视角）中学习语言时，表现远不如人类儿童。本文构建了Machine-DevBench基准测试，并证明VLM依赖强语义对齐的策展数据，无法利用自然主义弱对齐输入中的信号，为此提出EgoBabyVLM挑战以推动相关研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类儿童能从有限的、弱对齐的视觉-语言输入中稳健地习得语言，而现有VLM在自然主义自我中心视频（如可穿戴设备、婴儿头戴摄像头数据）上泛化能力差，且缺乏统一的评估标准来测量该领域的进展。

Method: 在具有不同语义对齐程度的数据集（包括自然主义婴儿和成人自我中心视频）上训练VLM，并设计覆盖多模态语言接地和单模态视觉/语言任务的综合评估套件。核心是Machine-DevBench基准，通过对数频率分箱从模型训练词汇中自动生成，消除训练/评估不匹配和统计效力低的问题。

Result: 当前VLM范式高度依赖策展数据的紧密语义对齐，无法利用在自然主义自我中心输入中占主导的弱对齐信号——而人类恰恰在这种信号环境下表现出色。

Conclusion: 提出了EgoBabyVLM挑战，旨在推动能像人类婴儿一样从自然主义数据中进行接地语言学习的模型开发。

Abstract: Children acquire language grounding with remarkable robustness from limited visuo-linguistic input in ways that surpass today's best large multimodal models. Recent research suggests current vision-language models (VLMs) trained on curated web data fail to generalize to the sparse, weakly-aligned egocentric streams produced by wearable devices, embodied agents, and infant head-cams -- and no fixed evaluation pipeline exists for measuring progress on this regime. We train VLMs on datasets with varying degrees of semantic alignment between visual and linguistic inputs, including naturalistic infant and adult egocentric videos, and evaluate them with a comprehensive suite spanning multimodal language grounding and unimodal vision and language tasks. At the core of this suite is Machine-DevBench, a corpus-grounded benchmark of lexical and grammatical competence, automatically generated from the model's training vocabulary across logarithmic frequency bins to eliminate the train/eval mismatch and low statistical power of prior developmental benchmarks. Our results show that current VLM paradigms hinge on the tight semantic alignment of curated data and fail to exploit the weakly-aligned signal that dominates naturalistic egocentric input -- the very regime in which humans thrive. To motivate progress, we introduce the EgoBabyVLM Challenge to drive the development of models capable of grounded language learning from the kind of naturalistic data that human infants experience.

</details>


### [100] [Stability and Discretization Error of State Space Model Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18905)
*Abderrahim Bendahi,Adrien Fradin,Johan Peralez,Julie Digne,Madiha Nadri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了神经算子离散化误差与稳定性的理论保证，推导了SS-NO和FNO的离散化误差界，并通过ISS分析和实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在求解PDE方面表现优异，但连续理论与离散数值实现之间的精确理论联系，特别是离散化误差与数值稳定性的关系，尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 通过理论推导建立离散化误差界，将解的正则性与输入离散化联系起来；针对SS-NO和FNO推导了新的离散化误差定理；利用输入到状态稳定性（ISS）分析评估离散化对连续域结果稳定性的影响；在1D和2D基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 证明了连接解正则性与输入离散化的解析界，为SS-NO和FNO提供了新的离散化误差定理；ISS分析揭示了离散化对稳定性的影响；实验验证了理论界的正确性及SS-NO在不同分辨率下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了神经算子连续理论与离散实现之间的理论鸿沟，为实际数值约束下的神经算子精度提供了形式化量化，证明了SS-NO在变分辨率下的稳健性。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as a powerful, discretization-invariant framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). Although established approaches like the Deep Operator Network (DeepONet) have successfully achieved universal approximation for operators, and architectures such as Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) have shown algebraic convergence rates, a precise theoretical connection between the continuous theory and its discrete numerical implementation remains a challenge. Specifically, the relationship between the continuous formulation and the discrete numerical stability has yet to be fully explored. In this paper, we address this gap by establishing theoretical guarantees for the discretization error and stability of neural operator approximation schemes. We prove analytical bounds that link solution regularity to input discretization, providing a formal quantification of neural operator accuracy under real-world numerical constraints. We derive these bounds to the specific cases of State Space Model-based Neural Operators (SS-NOs) and FNOs, thus providing a new discretization error theorem for these models. Additionally, through an input-to-state stability (ISS) analysis, we formally assess the impact of discretization on the stability of SS-NOs results obtained in the continuous domain. Our empirical experiments on 1D and 2D benchmarks validate our theoretical bounds and show the robustness of SS-NOs under varying resolutions.

</details>


### [101] [GRASP: Deterministic argument ranking in interaction graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19141)
*Diganta Misra,Antonio Orvieto,Rediet Abebe,Volkan Cevher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRASP框架，通过局部交互判断的聚合生成全局排名，替代不稳定的整体式LLM评判方法，并提供可审计的论证结构分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整体式LLM评判（holistic judging）在辩论评估中存在严重的跨模型不一致问题，因为将复杂的辩论交互结构压缩为单一模糊分数会导致不稳定性。

Method: 提出GRASP（Gradual Ranking with Attacks and Support Propagation），一个确定性框架，通过收敛的攻击-防御传播算子聚合稳定的局部交互判断，生成全局排名。

Result: 局部交互判断比整体式排名更具可重复性；GRASP生成的全局排名更一致；GRASP分数与人类"说服力"标签不相关。

Conclusion: GRASP不测量说服力、事实性或修辞吸引力，而是测量结构充分性——一种基于显式交互图的防御感知论证鲁棒性，可作为整体式LLM评判的透明、可审计替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as automated judges to evaluate the strength of arguments. As this role expands, their legitimacy depends on consistency, transparency, and the ability to separate argumentative structure from rhetorical appeal. However, we show that holistic judging - a common LLM-as-a-Judge practice where a model provides a global verdict on a debate - suffers from substantial inter-model disagreement. We argue that this instability arises from collapsing a debate's complex interaction structure into a single opaque score. To address this, we propose GRASP (Gradual Ranking with Attacks and Support Propagation), a deterministic framework that aggregates stable local interaction judgments into a global ranking via a convergent attack--defense propagation operator. We show that local interaction judgments are more reproducible than holistic rankings in LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations, allowing GRASP to produce more consistent global rankings. We further show that GRASP scores do not correlate with human "convincingness" labels, highlighting a vital sociotechnical distinction: GRASP does not measure persuasion, factuality, or rhetorical appeal, but structural sufficiency - a defense-aware notion of argument robustness over the explicit interaction graph. Overall, GRASP offers a transparent and auditable alternative to holistic LLM judging.

</details>


### [102] [Descriptive versus Regulatory Uncertainty in Bounded Predictive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18909)
*Ahmed Gamal Eldin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明当前Transformer架构在推理时仅具备“描述性不确定性”，即不确定性不会递归地影响系统策略。通过理论推导和3个规模（3B/8B/70B）的语言模型实验，发现token级香农熵在不同任务间统计不变（p≥0.568），而准确率差异显著（0%-100%），熵与准确率正交，且该结构缺陷无法通过增加参数或数据解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽承认压缩必有先验（即系统偏差），但未明确不确定性是否参与调控未来行为的动力学，抑或仅描述输出分布而无实际影响。作者希望区分这两种不确定性，并检验当前AI架构是否具备真正的认知锚定。

Method: 首先提出“描述性不确定性”（不递归调控策略）与“调控性不确定性”（直接进入优化景观并驱动自适应重构）的结构性区分。从形式上证明Transformer推理时局限于描述性不确定性，并通过朗道尔原理进行热力学论证。在3个本地部署的语言模型（3B、8B、70B参数）上进行token级香农熵与任务准确率的统计对比实验。

Result: 三个模型在所有任务中熵值统计不变（配对p≥0.568，模型内范围0.011-0.028 nats），但任务准确率在0%-100%间大幅变化。熵与准确率正交，且该解耦具有规模不变性：更大模型准确率更高，但熵平坦度完全相同。

Conclusion: 当前Transformer存在结构性的认知锚定缺陷，无法通过增加参数或训练数据解决。真正的认知锚定需要在热力学基底状态与信息处理成本之间建立物理耦合。

Abstract: Any system that models the world under finite representational capacity must compress; any compression entails a prior; and the prior is the system's bias. What has not been established is whether uncertainty participates in the dynamics governing future behavior, or merely describes the output distribution without consequence. We introduce a structural distinction between descriptive uncertainty, which does not recursively modulate the system's policy, and regulatory uncertainty, which directly enters the optimization landscape and drives persistent adaptive restructuring. We prove formally that current transformer architectures are confined to descriptive uncertainty at inference. We ground this in thermodynamics via Landauer's principle: for uncertainty to be regulatory, epistemic error must cost real energy; in a decoupled system, hallucinations and correct derivations dissipate identical energy. We test this empirically across three locally-deployed language models (3B, 8B, 70B parameters). Token-level Shannon entropy is statistically invariant across tasks spanning pattern retrieval, causal operator application, and out-of-distribution causal generalization in all three models (all pairwise p >= 0.568; within-model ranges 0.011-0.028 nats), while task accuracy varies substantially across the same conditions (0%-100%). Entropy and accuracy are orthogonal. The decoupling is scale-invariant: larger models achieve higher accuracy but identical entropy flatness. This structural incapacity is not resolvable by additional parameters or training data. Genuine epistemic grounding requires physical coupling between thermodynamic substrate state and information processing cost.

</details>


### [103] [CEPO: RLVR Self-Distillation using Contrastive Evidence Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19436)
*Ahmed Heakl,Abdelrahman M. Shaker,Youssef Mohamed,Rania Elbadry,Omar Fetouh,Fahad Shahbaz Khan,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对比证据策略优化（CEPO），通过正反答案双重对比强化关键推理步骤的奖励信号，在多模态数学推理基准上超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中正确解的每个token获得相同奖励，无法区分决定性推理步骤与语法填充词，现有方法要么导致答案信息泄漏，要么信号太弱。

Method: CEPO在每个token上同时问两个问题：正确答案是否偏好它？错误答案是否不偏好它？同时满足的token被视为真正推理步骤。错误答案来自训练批次中的被拒绝轨迹，无需额外采样成本。

Result: 在2B和4B规模下，五个多模态数学推理基准平均准确率分别达43.43%和60.56%，优于GRPO的41.17%和57.43%。分布匹配自蒸馏方法（OPSD, SDPO）甚至低于未训练基线。

Conclusion: CEPO继承了先前最优方法的结构安全保障，同时严格增强了关键token的信用分配，填充位置改进为零。

Abstract: When a model produces a correct solution under reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), every token receives the same reward signal regardless of whether it was a decisive reasoning step or a grammatical filler. A natural fix is to condition the model on the correct answer as a teacher, identifying tokens it would have generated differently had it known the answer. Prior work shows this either corrupts training by leaking the answer into the gradient, or produces a weak signal that cannot distinguish decisive steps from filler, since both look equally surprising relative to the model's baseline. We propose Contrastive Evidence Policy Optimization (CEPO), which asks a sharper question at every token: not just "does the correct answer favor this token?" but "does the correct answer favor it while the wrong answer disfavors it?" A token satisfying both is a genuine reasoning step; one satisfying neither is filler. The wrong-answer teacher is constructed from rejected rollouts already in the training batch, incurring no additional sampling cost. We prove CEPO inherits all structural safety guarantees of the prior state of the art while strictly sharpening credit at decisive tokens, with the improvement vanishing exactly at filler positions. Empirically, CEPO achieves 43.43% and 60.56% average accuracy across five multimodal mathematical reasoning benchmarks at 2B and 4B scale, respectively, versus 41.17% and 57.43% for GRPO under identical training budgets. Distribution-matching self-distillation methods (OPSD, SDPO) fall below the untrained baseline, empirically confirming the information leakage our theory predicts. Our code is available at https://github.com/ahmedheakl/CEPO.

</details>


### [104] [Does Your Wildfire Prediction Model Actually Work, or Just Score Well?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18911)
*Yangshuang Xu,Yuyang Dai,Liling Chang,Qi Wang,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个专用于野火预测的基础模型WILDFIRE-FM，并引入固定合同评估框架，通过十种Earth-FM基线对比，揭示了评估设计对迁移结论的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球基础模型针对通用大气和地球物理目标预训练，缺乏专门针对野火预测的模型；同时野火事件时空稀疏，评估设置对迁移结论高度敏感，缺乏标准化的评估框架。

Method: 提出WILDFIRE-FM，基于天气、活跃火观测、地形、植被和静态环境数据预训练；并设计固定合同评估框架，包含固定输出检查（匹配规则效应）和固定特征检查（头部选择效应），与十种Earth-FM基线对比。

Result: 在占用、扩散、检索和回归任务上的实验表明，野火迁移结论高度依赖评估设计和任务设定。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来野火专用的地球基础模型研究和基准测试提供了基础框架。

Abstract: Wildfire prediction is important for early warning and resource allocation, yet existing Earth foundation models (Earth FMs) are pretrained for general atmospheric and geophysical objectives rather than wildfire forecasting. To address this gap, we introduce WILDFIRE-FM, the first foundation model pretrained specifically for wildfire prediction using weather, active-fire observations, topography, vegetation, and static environmental data. However, introducing a domain-specific backbone alone does not solve the evaluation problem: wildfire events are sparse in space and time, making transfer conclusions highly sensitive to matching rules and evaluation settings. To address this problem, we introduce a fixed-contract evaluation framework with two controlled checks: a fixed-output check for matching-rule effects and a fixed-feature check for head-selection effects. Under matched contracts, we compare WILDFIRE-FM with ten Earth-FM baselines across occupancy, spread, retrieval, and regression tasks. Our results show that wildfire transfer conclusions depend strongly on evaluation design and task formulation. We hope this framework and WILDFIRE-FM provide a foundation for future wildfire-specific Earth-FM research and benchmarking. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Wildfire-fm-evaluation-contracts-5AE9/.

</details>


### [105] [HypergraphFormer: Learning Hypergraphs from LLMs for Editable Floor Plan Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18932)
*Nikita Klimenko,Hesam Salehipour,Parham Eftekhar,Amir Khasahmadi,Ramon Elias Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HypergraphFormer，一种基于超图表示和大语言模型的楼层平面图生成方法，通过监督微调生成编码空间关系的超文本文表示，在RPLAN数据集上优于现有方法，并展现出良好的数据效率、泛化能力和可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于光栅化或向量化表示的楼层平面图生成方法存在局限性，需要更高效、更灵活且支持任意不规则边界的生成方案。

Method: 提出HypergraphFormer，利用大语言模型通过监督微调学习超图表示，将空间关系和连接信息编码为文本化表示，从而生成楼层平面图。

Result: 在RPLAN数据集及另发布的分布外数据集上，该方法在多项指标上优于现有最先进方法，并展现出更好的数据效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 超图公式化方法通过解耦公寓足迹与功能/几何细分，支持任意不规则边界的楼层平面图生成，且具备高可编辑性，适合基于LLM的设计工作流。

Abstract: In this work, we propose HypergraphFormer, a novel and efficient approach to floor plan generation based on learning hypergraph representations with a large language model (LLM). The model is trained via supervised fine-tuning to generate a hypergraph-based textual representation that encodes spatial relationships and connectivity information within floor plans. We train and evaluate our approach on the RPLAN dataset, and further demonstrate its generalizability on a separate out-of-distribution dataset, which we release in this paper. Our method outperforms state-of-the-art techniques based on rasterized or vectorized representations across a diverse set of metrics. We also show improved data efficiency, particularly under distribution shift. The hypergraph formulation enables the generation of floor plans for arbitrary, irregular, user-specified boundaries by decoupling apartment footprints from their functional and geometric subdivisions. Furthermore, we show that the proposed methodology offers a high degree of editability, making it particularly well suited to design-oriented workflows supported by LLMs.

</details>


### [106] [A Geometric Analysis of Sign-Magnitude Asymmetry in a ReLU + RMSNorm Block under Ternary Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18933)
*Lei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过几何解释揭示了Pre-norm Transformer（使用RMSNorm）中符号扰动（量化误差）比幅度扰动产生约2.75倍更大输出能量的原因，并指出实际模型中该倍数放大的偏差源于离群特征破坏了局部化假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何Pre-norm Transformer对三元量化（{-1,0,+1}）表现出惊人的鲁棒性，从几何角度分析符号扰动与幅度扰动的不对称性。

Method: 采用符号-幅度分解方法，在两层ReLU+RMSNorm模型（i.i.d.高斯权重）中推导输出能量比（定理3）；分析符号量化误差作为保符号扰动的性质（定理4）；引入离群特征的能量放大因子R ≈ nα²解释实际模型偏差。

Result: 符号翻转产生约2.75倍于幅度扰动的横向输出能量；ReLU对三元误差近似透明（前后径向分数误差仅0.4%）；在TinyLlama-1.1B上线性响应区NLL杠杆稳定在~10×，符合每条目理论；离群特征α中位数0.024、最大值0.26证实重尾分布。

Conclusion: Bussgang常数2/π、RMSNorm几何结构和ReLU半空间结构共同解释了Pre-norm模型中的符号-幅度不对称性，R ∝ nα²解释了实际模型与理论的偏差。

Abstract: Pre-norm Transformers with RMSNorm tolerate ternary {-1,0,+1} weight quantization with surprisingly small loss (Ma et al., 2024). We give a geometric explanation via sign-magnitude decomposition of weight perturbations. In a two-layer ReLU + RMSNorm model with i.i.d. Gaussian weights, sign-flips produce $π/(π-2) \approx 2.75$ times more transverse output energy than sign-preserving magnitude perturbations of equal Frobenius norm, as the flip rate $p \to 0$ (Theorem 3). The mechanism: ReLU creates a hidden-space directional asymmetry between the two perturbation types, which RMSNorm's transverse-projection Fréchet derivative selectively exposes. Sign-quantization error is itself a sign-preserving perturbation with angular alignment $\cos^2 \to 2/π$ (Theorem 4); its post-ReLU radial fraction ($0.365$) matches the pre-ReLU value $1-2/π$ within $0.4\%$, so ReLU is approximately transparent to ternary error. Multi-layer compounding of the $2.75\times$ factor is not experimentally supported; the gap to real-model sign sensitivity arises from outlier features violating delocalization. For an input dimension with amplitude $α$, a single sign-flip produces post-ReLU energy amplified by $R \approx nα^2$ relative to a delocalized entry. On TinyLlama-1.1B, at linear response ($p \leq 0.5\%$), count-matched NLL leverage stabilizes at $\sim 10\times \approx n\mathbb{E}[α^2]$, matching the per-entry theory; the all-column NLL ratio of $5.0\times$ falls within $R_{\mathrm{col}} \leq 19$ ($67\times$ PPL gap reflects metric nonlinearity). Measured outlier $α$ at layer 12 (median $0.024$, max $0.26$) confirms heavy-tailed concentration. The Bussgang constant $2/π$, RMSNorm geometry, and ReLU half-space structure together explain sign-magnitude asymmetry in pre-norm models, with $R \propto nα^2$ accounting for real-model deviations.

</details>


### [107] [OScaR: The Occam's Razor for Extreme KV Cache Quantization in LLMs and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19660)
*Zunhai Su,Rui Yang,Chao Zhang,Yaxiu Liu,Yifan Zhang,Wei Wu,Jing Xiong,Dayou Du,Xialie Zhuang,Yulei Qian,Yuchen Xie,Yik-Chung Wu,Hongxia Yang,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OScaR提出了一种轻量级的KV缓存压缩框架，通过Canalized Rotation和Omni-Token Scaling解决Token Norm Imbalance问题，在INT2量化下实现近无损性能，解码速度提升3.0倍，内存减少5.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存的内存占用已成为长上下文推理和多模态LLM部署的主要瓶颈，而现有per-channel量化在极端压缩下效果不佳。

Method: 提出Token Norm Imbalance (TNI)是量化保真度的主要瓶颈。OScaR框架包含Canalized Rotation和Omni-Token Scaling两步，有效缓解TNI导致的序列维度方差问题，并辅以优化的系统设计和CUDA内核。

Result: 在X-LLMs上全面优于现有方法，INT2量化下实现近无损性能。相比BF16 FlashDecoding-v2基线，解码加速3.0倍，内存减少5.3倍，吞吐量提升4.1倍。

Conclusion: OScaR是一个鲁棒、低复杂度、通用的KV缓存压缩框架，定义了新的Pareto前沿，代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid advancement toward long-context reasoning and multi-modal intelligence has made the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache a dominant memory bottleneck for efficient deployment. While the established per-channel quantization effectively accommodates intrinsic channel-wise outliers in Key tensors, its efficacy diminishes under extreme compression. In this work, we revisit the inherent limitations of the per-channel quantization paradigm from both empirical and theoretical perspectives. Our analysis identifies Token Norm Imbalance (TNI) as the primary bottleneck to quantization fidelity. We demonstrate that TNI systematically amplifies errors when shared quantization parameters are required to span token groups exhibiting substantial norm disparities. Instead of relying on intricate quantization pipelines (e.g., TurboQuant), we propose OScaR (Omni-Scaled Canalized Rotation), an accurate and lightweight KV cache compression framework for X-LLMs (i.e., text-only, multi-modal, and omni-modal LLMs). Advancing the per-channel paradigm, OScaR employs Canalized Rotation followed by Omni-Token Scaling to mitigate TNI-induced sequence-dimensional variance both effectively and efficiently, further supported by our optimized system design and CUDA kernels. Extensive evaluations across X-LLMs show that OScaR consistently outperforms existing methods and achieves near-lossless performance under INT2 quantization, establishing it as a robust, low-complexity, and universal framework that defines a new Pareto front. Compared with the BF16 FlashDecoding-v2 baseline, our OScaR implementation achieves a notable up to 3.0x speedup in decoding, reduces memory footprint by 5.3x, and increases throughput by 4.1x. The code for OScaR is publicly available at https://github.com/ZunhaiSu/OScaR-KV-Quant.

</details>


### [108] [Shaping the Prior: How Synthetic Task Distributions Determine Tabular Foundation Model Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18971)
*Mohamed Bouadi,Nassim Bouarour,Varun Kulkarni,Shivam Dubey,Aditya Tanna,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出O'Prior，一个用于表格基础模型的合成先验构建框架，通过组合分层SCM生成器、模块化现实主义引擎、显式压力模块和课程化生成协议，显著提升模型在下游任务中的准确性和鲁棒性，证明合成先验构建是影响表格模型质量的关键但被忽视的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型的质量几乎完全取决于合成先验分布的设计，但现有合成先验过于理想化，缺少真实数据中的不规则性和失败模式，导致模型在部署时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出O'Prior框架，包含四个耦合组件：(1) 分层SCM元生成器，覆盖多种功能家族；(2) 模块化现实主义引擎，处理异质边缘分布、缺失值和目标变换；(3) 显式压力模块，注入混淆变量和查询分布不匹配；(4) 课程化、无泄漏生成协议。在固定架构、优化器和计算预算的条件下，仅改变合成任务分布来隔离先验设计的影响。

Result: O'Prior在真实表格基准上持续显著提升下游准确性和鲁棒性，增益集中在具有分布不规则性的场景。消融实验证实机制多样性、现实主义组合和偏移感知压力各自独立贡献，效果不可互换。

Conclusion: 合成先验构建是决定表格基础模型质量的一阶因素，但此前在很大程度上被忽视了。机制多样性、现实主义组合和显式压力注入是提升模型性能的关键设计维度。

Abstract: What determines the quality of a tabular foundation model? Unlike language or vision, tabular foundation models acquire their inductive biases almost entirely from synthetic pretraining distributions, yet the design of these distributions remains poorly understood. Standard synthetic priors are too well-behaved: they omit the irregularities and failure modes that determine deployment robustness. We introduce O'Prior, a compositional realism prior built around four coupled components: a hierarchical SCM meta-generator spanning diverse functional families; a modular realism engine covering heterogeneous marginals, missingness, and target transforms; an explicit stress module injecting confounding and support-query mismatch; and a curriculum-governed, leakage-safe generation protocol. To isolate prior design as the scientific variable, we hold architecture, optimizer, and compute budget fixed and vary only the synthetic task distribution. O'Prior yields consistent and substantial improvements in downstream accuracy and robustness across real tabular benchmarks, with gains concentrated in regimes characterized by distributional irregularities. Ablations confirm that mechanism diversity, realism composition, and shift-aware stress each contribute independently, their effects are not interchangeable. These results establish synthetic prior construction as a first-order and largely overlooked determinant of tabular foundation model quality

</details>


### [109] [Distilling Linearized Behavior for Effective Task Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18993)
*Thomas Sommariva,Francesca Morandi,Simone Calderara,Angelo Porrello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过知识蒸馏方法，将曲率正则化的线性化教师模型的表示能力迁移到非线性学生模型中，使得学生模型在保持标准微调效率的同时，具备线性化模型的任务向量可组合性，无需增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性微调产生的任务向量具有自然解缠和抗干扰的优点，但线性化模型在训练时表达能力受限且推理计算成本高，限制了实际应用。本文旨在弥合线性微调与标准非线性微调之间的差距。

Method: 在参数空间中与权重扰动相关的线性性质，可以通过在训练时对激活空间施加约束来实现。具体地，从曲率正则化的线性化教师模型中蒸馏隐藏表示到通过常规微调训练的非线性学生模型中。

Result: 所得模型继承了线性化模型在任务算术方面的关键特性，能够有效组合任务向量，在视觉和语言基准测试中均取得强性能，且不产生任何推理开销。

Conclusion: 通过激活空间的约束可以在保持标准微调效率的同时，使非线性模型获得线性化模型的任务向量可组合性，有效弥合了线性与非线性微调之间的差距。

Abstract: Task vector composition has emerged as a promising paradigm for editing pre-trained models, enabling model merging through addition and unlearning through subtraction. Fine-tuning in the tangent space of a pre-trained model (linear fine-tuning) has proven effective, as it produces task vectors that are naturally disentangled and resistant to interference. However, linearized models suffer from limited expressivity during training and incur higher computational costs at inference time, which restrict their practical applicability. In this work, we bridge the gap between linear and standard non-linear fine-tuning. We show that linearity with respect to weight perturbations, a property defined in parameter space, can be enforced through constraints in activation space during training. Concretely, we distill hidden representations from a curvature-regularized linearized teacher into a non-linear student trained via conventional fine-tuning. We find that the resulting model inherits key properties of linearized models for task arithmetic, enabling effective composition of task vectors and achieving strong performance across vision and language benchmarks without incurring any inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [110] [A Measure-Theoretic Analysis of Reasoning: Structural Generalization and Approximation Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19944)
*Yuyang Zhang,Yifu Zhang,Xuehai Zhou,Xiaoyin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过最优传输理论形式化推理过程，利用Wasserstein-1距离量化领域偏移，揭示了OOD泛化的两个主要约束：位置相关注意力机制缺乏平移不变性导致Lipschitz常数和期望风险下界为Ω(1)，以及TC^0 Transformer在Dyck-k语言上的电路深度下界，证明深度扩展是避免表示坍缩的必要条件而宽度扩展无法绕过。54种配置的实验验证了风险随Wasserstein领域偏移单调递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的经验性缩放定律已被广泛研究，但OOD泛化的理论机制仍不清楚，本文旨在通过最优传输理论建立形式化的理论框架来解释OOD泛化行为。

Method: 将离散推理轨迹投影到连续度量空间，利用Wasserstein-1距离量化领域偏移；通过Kantorovich对偶性将OOD泛化界与架构Lipschitz连续性及函数逼近极限联系起来；通过Dyck-k语言映射建立电路深度下界。

Result: 位置相关注意力机制（如绝对位置编码）导致Ω(1)的Lipschitz常数和期望风险；平移不变机制（如旋转位置编码）保持等变性并限制误差。TC^0 Transformer在Dyck-k语言上存在严格的电路深度下界，增加深度是必要的，增加宽度无法绕过Barron空间中的不可约逼近界。54种配置实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: Transformer的OOD泛化能力受限于位置编码的平移不变性和电路深度，深度扩展比宽度扩展更关键。

Abstract: While empirical scaling laws for LLM reasoning are well-documented, the theoretical mechanisms governing out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remain elusive. We formalize reasoning via optimal transport, projecting discrete trajectories into a continuous metric space to quantify domain shifts using the Wasserstein-1 distance. Invoking Kantorovich duality, we bound OOD generalization via architectural Lipschitz continuity and functional approximation limits. This exposes two primary constraints. First, position-dependent attention (e.g., Absolute Positional Encoding) fails to preserve shift invariance, yielding an $Ω(1)$ Lipschitz constant and expected risk, whereas shift-invariant mechanisms (e.g., Rotary Embeddings) preserve equivariance and bound the error. Second, by mapping sequential backtracking to a Dyck-$k$ language, we establish a strict circuit depth lower bound for $\text{TC}^0$ Transformers. Scaling physical layer depth is necessary to avert representation collapse -- a constraint that scaling representation width cannot bypass due to irreducible approximation bounds in Barron spaces. Evaluations across 54 Transformer configurations on combinatorial search corroborate these bounds, demonstrating that generalization risk degrades monotonically with the Wasserstein domain shift.

</details>


### [111] [Distance-Aware Muon: Adaptive Step Scaling for Normalized Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18999)
*Yury Demidovich,Abhishek Chakraborty,Grigory Malinovsky,Angelia Nedić,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种自适应缩放规则的Muon优化器变体（Distance-Adaptive Muon、Scale-Calibrated Muon和Distance-Free Muon），分别适用于非凸光滑目标和星凸目标，在理论上证明了收敛性保证，并在Transformer语言建模和图像分类任务上验证了其减少超参数调优敏感性的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon等归一化优化器将更新方向与步长解耦，但实际性能对归一化步长的尺度仍然敏感，需要自适应缩放规则来减少手动调参需求。

Method: 1) Distance-Adaptive Muon：根据轨迹探索的半径设置信任域半径，适用于光滑非凸目标；2) Scale-Calibrated Muon：保留指数移动平均，根据当前梯度和动量计算的局部下降证书设置步长，适用于星凸目标，证明O(1/T)目标间隙界；3) Distance-Free Muon：使用标量距离证书和主化一维搜索选择信任域半径，无需已知到全局最优解的距离。

Result: 在GPT-124M/WikiText-103的Transformer语言建模和ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100的图像分类实验中，提出的自适应缩放规则减少了对手动尺度调优的敏感性，在测试预算下匹配或超越了调优后的固定尺度Muon基线。
